### [36] [Can LLMs Judge Better Than They Generate? Evaluating Task Asymmetry, Mechanistic Interpretability and Transferability for In-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28050)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文挑战了"评估比生成更容易"这一核心假设。通过在受控上下文QA环境中测试，发现生成准确率在四项基准测试中有三项超过自我评估，仅多跳MuSiQue例外。注意力分析显示评估对上下文的关注度比生成低3-5倍，且几乎不阅读候选答案。LoRA微调证实这种不对称性并非训练假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM-as-a-Judge和自我评估管线中隐含的"评估比生成更容易"这一核心假设是否成立，并探究评估与生成之间的不对称性及其根源。

Method: 在受控的上下文问答设置中（上下文段落为唯一信息源），让模型评估自己生成的答案，以排除开放领域比较中参数化知识的混杂因素。在SQuAD 2.0、DROP、HotpotQA、MuSiQue四个基准上使用两种模型进行实验，并结合注意力分析和LoRA微调实验。

Result: 生成准确率在四项基准中有三项超过自我评估（仅多跳MuSiQue例外）；评估对上下文的注意力比生成少3-5倍，且几乎不阅读候选答案；LoRA微调显示生成微调导致过度接受，评估微调会损害生成能力。

Conclusion: 评估并不普遍比生成更容易，自我评估管线中存在显著的不对称性，这对LLM-as-a-Judge和自我评估框架的核心假设提出了挑战。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge and self-evaluation pipelines implicitly assume that evaluation is easier than generation. We test this in a controlled in-context QA setting where a context passage is the sole information source and each model judges the answer it generated, removing the parametric-knowledge confound of open-domain comparisons. Across four benchmarks (SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue) and two models, evaluation is not uniformly easier: generation accuracy exceeds self-evaluation on three of four, with multi-hop MuSiQue the exception. Attention analysis reveals why: evaluation attends to context 3--5x less than generation does and barely reads the candidate answer. LoRA fine-tuning confirms the asymmetry is not a training artifact: generation fine-tuning induces over-acceptance and evaluation fine-tuning degrades generation. These findings challenge core assumptions in self-evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [37] [MultiHashFormer: Hash-based Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28057)
*Huiyin Xue,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiHashFormer提出了一种基于哈希签名的自回归语言模型框架，将每个token表示为多个独立哈希函数生成的短哈希ID序列，通过哈希编码器和解码器实现参数高效的token表示，在100M到3B参数规模上持续优于标准Transformer LM，并支持多语言词汇扩展而无需增加参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型的嵌入矩阵随词汇表大小线性增长，导致参数量过大。虽然已有研究在编码器模型中采用哈希将多个token映射到同一向量以节省参数，但哈希碰撞使得该方法无法用于因果语言模型。因此，需要一种既能保持参数效率又能支持自回归生成的新方法。

Method: 每个token由多个独立哈希函数生成一个独特的哈希签名（短哈希ID序列）。Hash Encoder将该签名压缩为单个潜变量供Transformer解码器处理，然后Hash Decoder生成下一个token的哈希签名，再映射回文本。这一设计避免了哈希碰撞，使得自回归生成成为可能。

Result: 在100M、1B和3B参数规模上，MultiHashFormer在多个基准测试中一致优于标准Transformer LM。此外，模型支持多语言词汇扩展，且参数量保持不变，无需任何修改。

Conclusion: MultiHashFormer通过哈希签名机制有效解决了哈希碰撞问题，实现了参数高效的因果语言模型，在多个规模上表现优于标准Transformer，并具有天然的多语言可扩展性。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) represent tokens using embedding matrices that scale linearly with the vocabulary size. To constrain the parameter footprint, prior work proposes hashing many tokens into a single vector within encoder-only models. While this offers parameter efficiency, many-to-one collisions prevent its use in causal LMs. In this paper, we propose MultiHashFormer, a new framework that allows hash-based autoregression. Each token is represented as a unique hash signature, a short sequence of discrete hash IDs, generated by multiple independent hash functions. A Hash Encoder compresses this signature into a single latent vector for processing by a Transformer decoder. Then, a Hash Decoder generates the hash signature of the next token, which is then mapped back to text. We evaluate our approach at the 100M, 1B and 3B parameter scales, demonstrating that MultiHashFormer consistently outperforms standard Transformer LMs across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, we show that our model handles multilingual vocabulary expansion with a constant parameter footprint without any modifications.

</details>


### [38] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于机制驱动的训练稳定性监测方法，通过分析关键模块（低精度注意力、MoE路由器）的功能角色，在故障导致损失发散前数千步就能检测到异常信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练消耗巨大算力且耗时漫长，一旦发生数值或超参数故障，训练可能在损失和梯度范数看似正常的情况下持续数千步而不被发现，最终导致训练崩溃，造成巨大成本损失。

Method: 从每个关键模块的功能角色出发推导内部监测器，在故障预期产生可测量信号的最早计算位置进行检测。针对低精度Flash Attention，监测QK双线性分解的谱熵（其一阶项在损失完全崩溃前即出现异常）；针对MoE路由器，从其专家选择功能推导指标。

Result: 在低精度注意力、大学习率和组合故障的注入实验中，这些信号为不同故障提供了独特的特征签名，能够在损失发散前数千步触发预警。

Conclusion: 基于机制驱动的监测方法能够早期检测训练不稳定性，相比依赖损失和梯度范数的传统方法，可以提前数千步发现故障，为大规模模型训练提供了更有效的稳定性保障。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur. After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [39] [From Tokens to States: LLMs as a Special Case of World Models and the Continuous Path Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28127)
*Paul Dubois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文反对将LLM与世界模型视为二元对立，提出LLM是世界模型的退化特例，并论证从NTP到JEPA存在连续谱系，同时指出沿此谱系移动将失去LLM可规模化训练的两大优势（互联网自监督数据和专为离散token预测设计的Transformer架构），将此作为开放性研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI社区将LLM与世界模型的关系框架化为二元对立（LLM预测token，世界模型模拟现实），LeCun等人主张放弃自回归token预测转向潜在空间架构。作者认为这种框架过于二元化，试图消除这种对立，建立两者之间的连续统一视角。

Method: 理论论证两个核心主张：(1) 将LLM建模为世界模型的退化特例——状态空间为所有token序列，动作仅为追加一个token，因此世界模型是LLM的严格泛化；(2) 构建从NTP到JEPA的连续谱系，中间依次为多token预测、未来摘要预测、下一潜在预测等已有研究的中间态，逐步放松LLM约束。

Result: 提出了LLM与世界模型之间并非非此即彼的二元关系，而是一个连续谱系；识别出沿谱系移动时面临的两大开放问题：数据问题（从自监督文本到有标注动作环境的落差）和架构问题（Transformer能否泛化到连续状态预测）。

Conclusion: LLM和世界模型之间不存在根本性对立，而是可以统一在一个连续框架下理解。从NTP到JEPA的过渡虽然理论上更接近通用智能，但会逐步丧失LLM可规模化训练的两大实践优势，这构成了未来研究的关键挑战。

Abstract: The AI community has framed the relationship between large language models (LLMs) and world models as a dichotomy: LLMs predict tokens; world models simulate reality. Yann LeCun argues in 2022 that reaching general intelligence requires abandoning autoregressive token prediction in favour of latent-space architectures. This framing is unnecessarily binary. Two claims will be defended. First, LLMs are a degenerate special case of world models: the state space is the set of all token sequences, the only action is appending one token, and world models are therefore a strict generalisation of LLMs, not a replacement. Second, there is a natural continuous spectrum from NTP to JEPA, with multi-token prediction, future-summary prediction, and next-latent prediction as intermediate stations already populated by current research. Moving along this spectrum relaxes the LLM constraints one by one. It also progressively surrenders the two practical advantages that make LLMs trainable at scale: internet-scale self-supervised data, and a transformer architecture co-designed for discrete token prediction. Both are examined as open research questions: the data question (the cliff from self-supervised text to instrumented action-labelled environments) and the architecture question (whether the transformer generalises to continuous-state prediction, or whether a new primitive is needed).

</details>


### [40] [Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Xinyue Zeng,Zhuochun Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Epi2Diff框架，通过将大型推理模型的推理轨迹映射为认知片段序列（包括推理规模、努力分配、状态转移等特征）来预测人类项目难度，在四个真实数据集上优于现有方法，并揭示了难题引发更费力、迭代和实现导向的片段动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有项目难度预测方法依赖昂贵的人工校准或仅利用文本表征，缺乏对认知过程的洞察。本文认为难度不仅是文本属性，更是问题解决负担的可观察结果，因此需要利用大型推理模型的结构化推理轨迹来建模难度。

Method: Epi2Diff框架将LRM的推理轨迹划分为认知片段序列（问题解决状态组），提取片段动态特征（推理规模、努力分配、状态转移），并与语义项目表征结合，用于人类难度预测。

Result: 在四个真实人类难度数据集上，Epi2Diff持续优于强基线方法。在SAT分类基准上，相比监督微调LLM基线，平均相对提升8.1%。分析表明难题引发更费力、迭代和实现导向的片段动态，而非仅仅更长的回答。

Conclusion: LRM推理轨迹中的认知片段为人类项目难度提供了可预测且可解释的过程表征，为教育评估中的推理模型应用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.

</details>


### [41] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过激活修补、组件消融和机制分析，发现VLM中视觉理解默认优先，而先验知识依赖少量（2.5-4.8%）集中在网络后半部分的因果注意力头。消融这些头可在68-96%的先验知识提示下将预测从知识驱动翻转为视觉驱动，揭示了视觉与知识冲突的不对称因果机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅从行为层面描述VLM在视觉与知识冲突时的表现，缺乏组件级别的因果解释，无法理解模型内部如何解决这类冲突。

Method: 结合三种粒度的激活修补（残差流、注意力头、MLP子层）、模型组件消融研究和机制分析，在三个VLM家族上进行实验。

Result: 视觉理解默认优先；先验知识依赖网络后半部分少量注意力头（2.5-4.8%）。消融这些头使68-96%的先验知识提示预测翻转，但仅改变0.8-7.5%的视觉预测，呈现不对称因果结构。识别出头分为路由头（调控信息流）和写入头（直接投影答案token到残差流）。

Conclusion: VLM中存在稀疏的因果电路来解决感知-知识冲突，该结构跨模型家族和规模一致，揭示了视觉与先验知识之间的不对称因果机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>

### [42] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了世界AI模型网络（AI-ModelNet）的概念，借鉴互联网发展理念，通过建立模型间的通路实现异构模型之间的互联互通、能力共享和协同推理，以解决大模型应用中的高成本和部署复杂性问题，并通过原型系统和应用案例验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在实际应用中面临训练成本高、部署复杂等挑战，促使模型向轻量级、私有化和领域专用化方向发展。随着异构模型的快速 proliferation 和广泛分布，如何实现它们之间的有效交互与协作成为亟待解决的关键瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴互联网的发展理念（计算机创造互联网，互联网赋能计算机价值），提出AI-ModelNet概念、愿景和系统架构。首先回顾单模型和多模型研究现状，然后阐述系统愿景和分层架构，通过原型系统和多样化应用案例验证框架可行性，最后讨论未来研究方向。

Result: 提出了AI-ModelNet这一新型范式，建立了模型间互联互通、能力共享和协同推理的体系架构，并通过原型系统验证了框架的可行性。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet作为一种新型范式，有望解决大模型间协作的瓶颈问题，为异构模型的互操作和协同工作提供了系统化的解决方案，未来需要进一步探索相关研究方向。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过在前沿LLM上操纵人格特质（宜人性），在三个任务领域（结构化编码、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判）中测量多智能体团队的表现。

Result: 人格效应关键取决于任务结构：编码任务中低宜人性引发巨大沟通变化但对里程碑完成影响很小；开放式协作和谈判中，相同操作显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统设计需考虑人格提示与任务结构的交互作用，人格操纵的效果具有任务依赖性。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>

Abstract: This article addresses the combinatorial complexity inherent in modern high-tech system design by presenting automation-in-design (AiD) as a transformative paradigm. Two case studies (e-drive system design and spatial dimensioning problem) serve as proof-points for this approach. The AI-driven methods used in the case studies represent a fundamental shift in engineering, advancing from simulation-based optimisation towards autonomous design with minimal human supervision.

</details>

*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练范式，通过在LLM智能体中注入、结构化并精炼前瞻性预测能力，实现真正的内部世界建模，从而克服标准智能体在长周期任务中仅能被动反应的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM智能体在长周期任务中本质上是被动反应的，缺乏人类那种通过"假设推演"来评估潜在计划的能力，因为它们没有内部世界模型来模拟未来结果。

Method: 提出三阶段训练方法：(i) 世界模型智能体中间训练(WM-AMT)注入潜在预测能力；(ii) 格式引导的监督微调(FE-SFT)结构化注入的能力；(iii) 前瞻条件强化学习(FC-RL)精炼生成模拟的校准与实用性。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务上，该方法持续优于其他训练基线。

Conclusion: 在LLM智能体中实现有效的内部世界建模需要"能力优先"的训练管线，以获得有根基且经过校准的前瞻性能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [46] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ODYSSEY——一个用于构建可验证、局部保真的基础模型的范畴论框架，通过称为"foundries"的构件式架构组件（覆盖了局部上下文、局部表示族、限制映射、胶合规则、阻碍策略、更新义务及人机交互视图）实现。通用基础学习（UFL）形式化了foundry的构建过程（左右Kan扩张），Foundry SQL提供查询接口，TICKET认证机制用于模型准入。该框架在多个领域实现并测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型缺乏局部保真性和可验证性，难以在关键领域中保证知识的局部一致性和全局协调性。作者希望提供一个范畴论形式化的框架，使得不同领域的知识能够以"foundries"为构件进行组合和验证，同时保持局部上下文的保真性。

Method: 采用范畴论中的层论（sheaf theory）作为核心框架，定义了foundry（构件式架构组件）包含局部上下文覆盖、表示族、限制映射、胶合规则、阻碍策略等组件。UFL使用左右Kan扩张来形式化foundry的构建与验证，FSQL提供类型化查询接口，TICKET使用因果Kan扩张变换器（Causal Kan Extension Transformers）进行认证。

Result: ODYSSEY已完全实现并在多个具体foundries（证据/论证、运营决策、机构/金融、市场意义、科学挑战、研究项目、助手构建、评估框架等）上测试，展示了领域构建、工件回放、层诊断、接地Toulmin/局部LLM审查、残余阻碍分类账及优化的因果声明提取等功能。

Conclusion: 相同的范畴论机制能够支持跨异构来源的多种知识工程任务，证明ODYSSEY是一个统一的、可验证的知识基础模型构建框架。该论文将在ICML 2026以2.5小时教程形式呈现。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [47] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DysLexLens，一个基于低资源LLM的框架，用于通过在线论坛（Reddit）分析诵读困难学习者使用AI工具的真实体验。该框架包含词典驱动过滤、知识图谱推理、定量评估（RAGAS、查询鲁棒性）和定性验证（幻觉与证据对齐）四大模块，并在30个问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诵读困难学习者越来越多地使用AI工具，但其真实体验（lived experiences）尚未被充分研究。现有方法难以从低资源、嘈杂的论坛数据中提取有效信息，亟需一个端到端、可追溯证据的分析框架。

Method: DysLexLens是一个四阶段端到端架构：(1) 词典驱动过滤（dictionary-driven filtering）构建聚焦的Reddit语料库；(2) LLM辅助语义分析结合知识图谱（KG）查询推理；(3) RAGAS和查询鲁棒性（Query Robustness）定量评估；(4) 基于幻觉和证据对齐的结构化定性验证指南。

Result: 使用Reddit论坛数据和30个问题验证了框架有效性，展示了其在其他低资源论坛数据上的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: DysLexLens能够有效从低资源论坛数据中分析诵读困难学习者与AI工具交互的真实体验，且具有可推广性和可复现性（代码与数据已开源）。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [48] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现显式推理虽能提升可解释性，但未必能提高多模态情感识别的准确率。基于此，提出MER-R1强化学习框架，通过将快思考（直接回答）与慢思考（推理后回答）的互补性转化为显式优化，利用双目标解耦和置信度校准，在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion上取得了最优性能，使推理真正服务于情感识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理型多模态大语言模型在情感识别中，快速直接回答往往比深思熟虑后的推理回答效果更好，表明显式推理不一定提升准确率。作者旨在揭示快慢思考的互补特性，并设计一种方法让两者协同优化，使推理真正有益于情感识别。

Method: 提出MER-R1强化学习框架，核心包括：(1) 双目标解耦——将召回率和精确率分离为两个优化信号，实现联合优化；(2) 快慢置信度校准——将慢思考的最终答案与快思考的直觉对齐，强化正确情感、抑制错误情感；并提供理论证明表明该协同优化可缓解优化过程中的方差诱导干扰。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion两个基准上，MER-R1达到了最优性能，实验表明快思考提升召回率（更广泛、更自信的预测），慢思考提升精确率（保守过滤错误类别），而MER-R1有效融合了两者优势。

Conclusion: 快慢思考具有互补性——快思考偏向召回，慢思考偏向精确。MER-R1通过强化学习框架将这种互补性转化为显式优化目标，使推理真正有益于多模态情感识别，并提供了理论依据证明了该方法在缓解优化干扰方面的有效性。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [49] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出树状证据推理框架（ToE），通过强化学习驱动的多源检索、证据评估和论证树聚合算法，实现层级化的事实核查。理论分析证明了检索策略收敛于信息论最优策略邻域，实验表明在对抗性毒化输入下性能提升4-24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成错误信息在生成式引擎优化（GEO）投毒下可系统性地被检索系统呈现，污染大语言模型推理，亟需自动化事实核查方法应对这一威胁。

Method: ToE框架将每个声明建模为动态扩展的论证树，集成三个核心组件：强化学习驱动的多源检索智能体、证据评估智能体、论证树聚合算法，通过可解释的证据链迭代地分解、检索和验证声明。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干LLM上，ToE相比强基线方法提升4-24个百分点，在对抗性毒化输入上增益尤为显著。理论分析给出了形式化误差界，证明学习策略收敛于信息论最优策略的邻域。

Conclusion: ToE通过层级证据推理有效提升自动化事实核查的准确性和鲁棒性，特别适用于对抗性错误信息的检测场景。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [50] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架，通过自然语言提示映射逻辑符号、符号验证器识别错误并生成修正指令、以及计划识别器推断目标可达性，显著提升了LLM在长程规划任务中的可行性和正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长程规划任务中常因固有复杂性产生不可行或错误的解决方案，其鲁棒性和可靠性存在严重安全关切，亟需提升其规划能力。

Method: 提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 自然语言提示机制，将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述；(2) 符号验证器，识别错误并转换为LLM可理解的修正指令；(3) 计划识别器，推断目标可达性以引导规划过程。

Result: 实验表明，所提框架在长程规划任务中持续提升了方案的可行性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效增强了基于LLM的规划可靠性，有望推动更可信赖的AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [51] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究图世界模型（GWM）中的长程 rollout 误差，提出了统一框架、图值 rollout 边界以及误差感知 GWM 方法，在合成和真实图基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多规划环境本质上是图结构（智能体、工具、技能、路线和依赖关系），但现有世界模型多针对向量或图像设计。图结构下的 rollout 误差传播机制尚不明确，需要理论分析和有效方法解决长程 rollout 发散问题。

Method: 1) 提出统一的固定边/动态边 GWM 框架，包含动作节点以支持节点、边和图级决策；2) 推导图值 rollout 边界，分离拓扑放大与模型放大效应；3) 引入联合节点-边算子用于动态边 rollout；4) 提出 Error-Aware GWM，结合谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异质智能体-图测试平台上，rollout 误差和规划遗憾随 horizon 增长；动态边训练在结构演化时不可或缺；Error-Aware GWM 能防止长程发散同时保持预测精度。真实图基准明确了 GWM 的适用范围：在动态图 rollout 和智能体规划上表现最佳，而专用图模型在静态或稀疏预测任务上仍具优势。

Conclusion: 图世界模型适用于动态图 rollout 和智能体规划，Error-Aware GWM 能有效控制长程误差发散，但静态图任务仍推荐使用专用图模型。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [52] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了语言智能体的两种世界模型（基于Agent和参数化），提出GILP方法，通过小型参数化骨干网络与LLM推理结合，利用一致性门控减少幻觉状态，在规划基准上显著提升成功率并降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Agent的世界模型灵活但易产生幻觉状态且难以量化误差；参数化世界模型误差可测但规划能力弱。两者各有优劣，需要结合两者优势以提升语言智能体在结构化规划任务中的表现。

Method: 提出GILP（Grounded Iterative Language Planning）：训练小型参数化骨干网络（提供有效动作、状态增量、风险和价值），结合API-based LLM推理（生成动作和想象增量），通过一致性门控机制在两者不一致时要求修订。

Result: 在GPT-4o-mini上，幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035；校准模拟器中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的LLM调用量。

Conclusion: 结合参数化世界模型的精确性和基于Agent世界模型的灵活性，能够有效减少幻觉并提升规划成功率，是一种高效且实用的混合方案。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [53] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATOD（退火轮次感知在线蒸馏）算法，通过退火式OPD-RL混合训练和轮次级重加权机制，在长周期交互任务中使小语言模型智能体超越教师模型，在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上平均成功率比OPD提升3.03点，比GRPO提升23.62点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在早期训练快速但收益随学生逼近教师而饱和；强化学习（RL）能直接优化环境奖励但有稀疏延迟反馈导致早期效率低。两者互补，需一种混合方法兼顾早期效率和最终性能上限。

Method: (1) 退火式OPD-RL调度：早期OPD主导快速逼近教师行为，后期逐步加强RL以驱动基于奖励的探索。(2) 轮次级分歧-不确定重加权（T-DUR）：在长轨迹中软放大高效用轮次，增强密集监督。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上，ATOD在三种学生模型规模下均一致优于竞争性后训练基线：平均成功率比OPD提升3.03点，比GRPO提升23.62点，且超过对应教师模型2.16点。

Conclusion: ATOD有效融合了OPD的早期快速学习和RL的探索优化能力，通过退火调度和轮次级重加权，不仅克服了OPD的性能饱和瓶颈，还实现了学生超越教师的跨越式改进。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [54] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NormAct是一个评估多模态大语言模型在具身社交场景中遵循隐含社会规范的基准。实验表明当前SOTA模型（GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro）在显式目标达成（67.3%）与隐式规范遵守（26.4%）之间存在显著差距，主要原因在于规范激活与情境化能力不足。作者提出NormPerceptor，一种基于上下文条件的线索生成器，能将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多模态大语言模型作为具身规划器时，通常聚焦于显式目标达成或直接规范知识测试，很少评估模型能否推断并在动作序列中应用隐含的社交规范。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建NormAct基准，将隐藏规范嵌入普通任务中评估模型；提出NormPerceptor，一种上下文条件线索生成器，在规划前推断场景相关规范。

Result: SOTA模型显式目标达成率67.3%，但隐式规范遵守率仅26.4%；线索提示实验表明差距源于规范激活与情境化困难；NormPerceptor将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。

Conclusion: 具身智能体需具备主动检测隐藏规范、将其可视证据情境化并整合为行动规划约束的能力，这是提升社交适切行为的关键。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [55] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SD-GPS框架，通过将符号求解器作为执行预言机，统一求解器驱动的自动形式化和验证性定理提出，显著提升了几何问题求解的神经符号推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号几何求解框架在自动形式化（多模态翻译与下游求解器脱节）和定理预测（固定规则库导致演绎困境）两大核心阶段存在严重瓶颈。

Method: 1) 求解器驱动的自动形式化：基于QwenVL3-2B，将有监督的形式语言适应和可解性引导的强化学习统一为单一模块，以可执行性为核心训练信号；2) 验证性定理提出：引入困境感知智能体，从当前证明状态提出局部辅助引理，通过符号验证过滤所有提议确保可靠性。

Result: 在Geometry3K和PGPS9K数据集上，SD-GPS在标准补全、多项选择和跨模态参考任务中一致优于现有MLLM、神经和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 闭环多模态感知与符号执行显著提升几何推理能力，为神经智能体通过形式系统锚定实现可验证的问题求解提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [56] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelBall模型扩展了Rotate3D，通过模变换建模层次结构（抽象概念模小、具体实例模大）和以尾实体为中心的关系球建模多种基数关系（1-1, 1-N, N-1, N-M），实现了对所有关系模式（对称、反对称、逆、组合、层次）的覆盖，在链接预测任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC模型如RotatE无法建模非交换组合模式，Rotate3D引入了非交换性但仍无法捕捉知识图谱中的语义层次，且两者都无法有效建模一对多关系。

Method: RelBall在Rotate3D基础上提出两项创新：(1) 模变换（modulus transformation），通过将抽象概念映射到小模、具体实例映射到大模来建模层次结构；(2) 以尾实体为中心的关系球（tail-centric relation ball），建模一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。

Result: 在多个数据集上的链接预测实验中，RelBall相比各类基线模型取得了有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: RelBall能够覆盖所有关系模式（对称、反对称、逆、组合及语义层次），提供可解释的层次化表示（模直接反映语义层级），并支持多种基数关系，有效弥补了现有模型的不足。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG）及其部分有向变体（PD-PCFG），并设计了提升因果推理（LCI）算法，能够在关系域中高效计算因果效应，相比命题级因果推理大幅提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将提升推理（lifted inference）技术从概率查询扩展到因果效应计算，以解决关系域中因果推断的计算效率问题，克服传统命题级因果贝叶斯网络在大规模关系数据上的性能瓶颈。

Method: (1) 提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG），将因果知识嵌入提升模型，并给出干预的形式化语义；(2) 设计LCI算法，在提升层面直接计算因果效应，避免展开为命题图；(3) 进一步提出PD-PCFG处理部分因果知识，并扩展LCI以适用于先验因果知识不完备的场景。

Result: LCI算法在提升层面计算因果效应，相比命题级因果推理（如因果贝叶斯网络）实现了显著的加速；PD-PCFG的扩展进一步放宽了对完整因果知识的要求，适用于更广泛的实际模型。

Conclusion: 提升推理可以有效地应用于关系域中的因果效应计算，PCFG和LCI为高效因果推断提供了新框架，PD-PCFG则增强了其在部分因果知识场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>


### [58] [JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070)
*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 京东推出的氧气AI商品中心（Oxygen AIIC），是一个基于大语言模型/视觉语言模型构建的工业级商品知识生产与服务平台，通过本体工程、S2D知识识别架构、自进化商品理解模型和统一商品通道四大支柱，解决了海量SKU场景下快速涌现概念、高质量知识生产和多样化下游需求三大挑战，实现了数十亿SKU的高效知识生产与服务，并在搜索、推荐、运营等核心场景取得显著业务提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 京东作为服务超7亿活跃用户、拥有数百亿SKU的电商平台，面临三大工业级挑战：快速涌现的新概念、海量SKU的高质量知识生产、以及多样化的下游需求，亟需一个大规模、高质量的结构化商品知识生产与服务平台。

Method: 提出了四大核心支柱：(i) 基于高效人机协同的本体工程，支持数百万条本体的动态演化与敏捷扩展；(ii) "语义搜索+判别"(S2D)知识识别架构，结合吞吐量优化策略，实现可扩展、高吞吐的AI商品库生产；(iii) 自进化的商品理解大模型，稳定可控地持续提升，知识生产精度达94.2%、召回率82.8%；(iv) 统一商品通道作为数据与服务枢纽。整个系统部署在华为昇腾NPU上。

Result: 覆盖数万京东类目，每日处理数亿商品更新，积累数千亿商品知识资产。搜索流量覆盖率80.4%，商品信息质量问题降低37%，商品上架核心属性自动填充率超80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC通过LLM/VLM驱动的工业级商品知识生产平台，有效解决了超大规模电商场景下的知识生产与服务难题，并在搜索、推荐、运营、类目规划等核心业务场景中取得了可量化的规模级收益。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [59] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPI框架，通过本体引导的关系图进行双向检索和迭代精炼，解决多跳KGQA中搜索空间膨胀和语义约束不满足的问题，在WebQSP、CWQ和MetaQA上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主题中心扩展的多跳KGQA方法面临两大挑战：搜索空间随噪声混合类型路径快速增长，且检索路径可能无法满足复杂问题的语义约束。

Method: 提出OPI框架，包含三个关键组件：1）关系中心本体图，捕获关系的头尾类型约束；2）双向检索机制，通过预测答案类型映射到兼容的最终跳关系，结合主题侧前缀扩展和答案侧最终跳匹配；3）迭代精炼策略，在问题上下文中重新评估检索路径和候选答案。

Result: 在WebQSP上Hit@1/F1提升4.6/5.0点，在CWQ上提升8.9/3.3点，在MetaQA上仅用检索模块即达到接近饱和的Hit@1。

Conclusion: OPI通过本体引导有效抑制噪声混合类型扩展，大幅减少搜索空间，显著提升了多跳KGQA的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [60] [Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28166)
*Difan Jiao,Raghav Singhal,Robert West,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL)，将双人训练范式引入基于可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)中，让更强的资深模型与冻结的初级模型随机交替协作生成推理过程并共享奖励。在竞赛数学任务上训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct，TRL在保持单模型推理能力的同时，实现了更强的交接鲁棒性、更小的分布漂移和更可读的思维链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能大幅提升大模型的推理能力，但会导致推理向可读性差、语言混杂等特化模式漂移，使得较弱的智能体甚至人类难以跟随和利用这种能力。现有双人训练范式仅在小规模概念验证中展示，尚未验证其能否扩展到现代RLVR的长思维链场景。

Method: TRL中，一个可训练的资深模型与一个冻结的初级模型随机交替共同生成推理过程，生成的完整推理获得奖励，然后将标准GRPO损失应用于资深模型。资深模型被激励以初级模型能够跟随的方式进行推理。

Result: 在竞赛数学任务上训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct，TRL在单模型推理能力上与vanilla GRPO持平，同时从相同的rollout结构中涌现出三个特性：与初级模型的更强交接鲁棒性、更小的分布漂移、以及初级模型更易读的思维链。

Conclusion: TRL展示了RLVR在多模型通信和人类兼容性方面具有实用价值的有前途的路线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.

</details>


### [61] [Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28270)
*Bo Shen,Lifeng Chang,Tianyuan Wei,Yunpeng Li,Feng Shi,Yichen Han,Peijie Gao,Shiyi Kuang,Xin Chang,Dehui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent原生免疫系统(ANIS)——首个受生物启发的、嵌入智能体认知循环的内生防御架构。ANIS包含六层免疫塔(L0-L5)、Agent病毒与疫苗统一分类体系、三重监控背骨(Harness Triad)和持续免疫学习(CIL)，并严格区分了模型对齐与Agent免疫：对齐提供静态“宪法”价值基础，ANIS扮演运行时动态“执法”机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制（如边界安全、训练时对齐）均位于智能体主动推理循环之外，无法防御运行时劫持（如内存投毒、工具链操纵、多智能体协议攻击）。需要一个嵌入智能体认知循环内部的内生防御架构。

Method: 1) 六层免疫塔(L0-L5)，其中L1为屏障免疫（非认知的物理与逻辑隔离层）；2) Agent病毒与疫苗统一分类体系，区分浅层非参数防御与鲁棒参数化疫苗；3) Harness Triad（元、自我、自动）——自监控元认知自动化骨干，驱动持续免疫学习(CIL)；4) 严格区分模型对齐与Agent免疫的理论边界。

Result: 提出了首个原生嵌入智能体推理循环的生物启发式内生防御架构，建立了Agent病毒/疫苗的统一分类学，设计了持续免疫学习机制，并给出了对齐与免疫的理论区分。

Conclusion: ANIS为智能体运行时安全提供了新范式。开放挑战包括：免疫协议标准化、自免疫率（假阳性干预率）等新评估指标、病原体与疫苗在集体智能生态系统中的共演化动力学。

Abstract: The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

</details>

### [62] [Cluster, Route, Escalate: Cascaded Framework for Cost-Aware LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27457)
*Yasmin Moslem,Magdalena Kacmajor,Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Ammar Abbas,Solmaz Panahi,David Lynch,Zhuangzhuang Nie,Alexandros Agapitos,Aleksandar Milenovic,Hongmeng Song,Yucheng Shi,Yue Pan,Patricia Buffini,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 提出两阶段级联系统：第一阶段根据查询聚类分配最经济的模型，第二阶段通过质量估计将低质量输出升级到更强模型，在保留97-99%最强模型精度的同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生产部署中准确性与成本之间存在权衡，单一模型要么对简单查询成本过高，要么对困难查询能力不足，需要一种能根据查询难度动态分配模型资源的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段级联方案：Stage 1对查询聚类并分配最经济的模型，通过可解释超参数离线调优控制成本预算；Stage 2引入质量估计(QE)级联，当Stage 1输出被判定为低质量时，将查询升级到更强的模型。

Result: 在测试数据集上，级联系统保留了最强模型97-99%的准确率，同时降低了每次输出token的时间(TPOT)。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需任务正确性标签，能够适应模型池的变化而无需手动重新配置，有效实现了准确性与推理成本的平衡。

Abstract: Efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs) in production forces a trade-off between accuracy and cost. Operators often default to a single model that is either expensive for easy queries or insufficient for hard ones. To address this challenge, we propose a two-stage cascaded solution. Stage 1 clusters incoming queries and assigns each cluster to its most cost-effective model. The cost budget for this routing process is set by an interpretable hyperparameter, tuned offline. Stage 2 adds a quality estimation (QE) cascade; when an output from Stage 1 is judged low-quality, the query is escalated to a stronger model. This ensures only hard or low-confidence cases reach the expensive models. On the test datasets, the cascaded system retains 97-99% of the strongest model's accuracy while reducing Time Per Output Token (TPOT). It requires only task-correctness labels and adapts to changes in the model pool without manual reconfiguration.

</details>


### [63] [Mixed-Precision For Energy Efficient Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27949)
*Gülçin Gedik,Robert Schöne,Roman Iakymchuk*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本研究探索混合精度计算策略，通过Reactor Simulator和LULESH两个基准测试验证，在不牺牲精度的情况下，分别实现了30%的时间和能耗缩减（Reactor Simulator），以及最高30%的时间缩减和25%的能耗缩减（LULESH）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模拟逼真度提高，高精度计算导致计算时间延长和能耗大幅增加，亟需寻找优化策略来平衡精度与性能。

Method: 采用混合精度计算方法，利用计算机算术工具优化性能，以Reactor Simulator和LULESH为案例进行基准测试评估。

Result: Reactor Simulator：时间和能耗均降低30%；LULESH：时间降低最高30%，能耗降低25%。

Conclusion: 混合精度计算在保持计算精度的前提下，能够有效减少大规模模拟的计算时间和能耗，是一种有前景的优化策略。

Abstract: As simulations grow more realistic, the pursuit of higher accuracy results in extended computation times and substantial power consumption. This study explores mixed-precision computing as a promising strategy to address these challenges, leveraging computer arithmetic tools to optimize performance. Using Reactor Simulator and LULESH benchmarks as case studies, we evaluated the potential of mixed-precision strategies to reduce both time-to-solution and energy-to-solution. For Reactor Simulator, we achieved a 30% reduction in both metrics without compromising accuracy. Similarly, for LULESH, results demonstrated up to a 30% improvement in time-to-solution and a 25% reduction in energy-to-solution.

</details>

### [64] [How far does a random forest generalize from a 54-run LAMMPS+SPICA benchmark?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27695)
*Dennis Alves Pedersen,Paulo Henrique Leme Ramalho,Fábio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探索使用冷启动随机森林代理模型，基于54次LAMMPS+SPICA粗粒化分子动力学模拟数据，预测混合MPI+OpenMP配置性能，在单一硬件体制内能正确排序配置，跨体制时性能下降，为减少基准测试开销提供了可信任区域图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上为分子动力学工作负载选择近优混合MPI+OpenMP配置需要大量经验性基准测试，消耗大量分配预算。本文旨在避免在集群上运行完整基准测试的前提下，利用冷启动代理模型预测性能并推荐高性能配置。

Method: 使用54次LAMMPS+SPICA运行（抗菌肽Tritrpticin在水合DOPC双层上的粗粒化模拟，4354个粗粒珠）构建训练数据集，涵盖1-8个AMD EPYC 7662节点上的18种混合配置（每种3次重复）。9个拓扑和资源特征输入5个回归器，预测循环时间和四个内部LAMMPS计时分数（Pair、Kspace、Comm、Modify）。采用留一维度的泛化测试评估跨体制预测能力。

Result: 样本内循环时间平均绝对误差0.49秒（相对误差4.0%）。特征重要性显示OpenMP线程数和MPI/OpenMP比例占主导，原始节点数和核心数贡献不足3%。留一维度泛化测试表明，预测准确性由硬件体制隶属关系支配：单节点、多节点或共享线程体制内代理模型能正确排序配置，跨体制时性能下降。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了代理模型推荐可信任的体制内可解释图谱，可在名义成本的一小部分内界定进一步基准测试活动的范围。

Abstract: Selecting near-optimal hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations for molecular dynamics workloads on modern HPC clusters has traditionally required exhaustive empirical benchmarking, consuming allocation budget proportional to the number of configurations evaluated. This work investigates whether a cold-start Random Forest surrogate, trained once on a small, structured benchmark dataset, can reliably predict execution performance and recommend high-performing configurations without further cluster runs. The training dataset comprises 54 LAMMPS+SPICA runs of the antimicrobial peptide Tritrpticin on a hydrated DOPC bilayer (4 354 coarse-grained beads), spanning 18 hybrid configurations on 1-8 AMD EPYC 7662 nodes of the Lovelace cluster at CENAPAD-SP, with three independent replications each. Nine topology and resource features feed five regressors that predict loop time and four internal LAMMPS timing fractions (Pair, Kspace, Comm, Modify). In-sample mean absolute error is 0.49 s on loop time (4.0 % relative). Feature importance localizes predictive signal in topology variables (OpenMP threads and MPI/OpenMP ratio dominate; raw node and core counts contribute under 3 %). Leave-one-dimension-out generalization reveals that accuracy is governed by hardware regime membership: within a common regime (single-node, multi-node, or shared threading tier) the surrogate ranks configurations correctly, and degrades when targets cross architectural boundaries. The result is an interpretable map of where the surrogate's recommendations can be trusted, useful for scoping further benchmark campaigns at a fraction of their nominal cost.

</details>


### [65] [Lightweight Multi-Vehicle Collaborative Perception Acceleration with Fusion Position Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27750)
*Wenzhao Zhang,Shujun Han,Haixiao Gao,Mengying Sun,Bizhu Wang,Xiaodong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对多车协同感知（MvCP）提出了一种基于线性层间特征融合位置调整（FALL）的轻量级加速方案，在保证理论精度无损失的前提下，可分别减少高达74.8%（通信资源受限）和30.3%（计算资源受限）的协同感知延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多车协同感知（MvCP）是自动驾驶的关键技术，但实时MvCP在有限的计算和通信资源下难以实现，现有方法无法同时适应两种资源受限场景，亟需一种轻量级加速方案。

Method: 1. 提出条件可加性的松弛定义，分析各类DNN线性层的条件可加性；2. 聚焦基于加性特征融合的中间融合MvCP，推导前向与后向特征融合位置（FP）调整在精度上的一致性；3. 提出线性层间FP调整（FALL）方案，理论上实现无损加速。

Result: 在通信资源受限场景下，MvCP延迟降低高达74.8%；在计算资源受限场景下，延迟降低高达30.3%。

Conclusion: FALL方案可在不损失精度的前提下显著加速中间融合多车协同感知，有效适应计算与通信资源双重受限的自动驾驶实际部署需求。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception (MvCP) is considered as a key technology to facilitate automated driving (AD), where real-time MvCP under limited resources is significant for reliable AD. In this paper, we formulate a lightweight acceleration scheme for intermediate-fusion (IF) MvCP, which can adapt to both situations of limited computation and communication resources. We provide a relaxed definition conditional additivity and analyze the conditional additivity for various DNN linear layers. On this basis, we focus on the IF-MvCP based on additive feature fusion, and derive the MvCP precision consistency of the forward and backward feature fusion position (FP) adjustments among linear layers. Through experiments, we further validate the precision consistency of the FP adjustment method. Moreover, we propose an FP adjustment among linear layers (FALL) scheme for MvCP acceleration without precision loss theoretically. Simulation results show that the proposed FALL can reduce MvCP latency by up to 74.8% under limited communication resources and by up to 30.3% under limited computation resources.

</details>


### [66] [Optimizing Teacher-Student Partitioning for Scalable Knowledge Distillation on HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27797)
*Adrian P. Dieguez,Victor Conchello Vendrell,Alex Batlle,Vinnam Kim,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种面向HPC的知识蒸馏方法，通过解耦教师和学生模型的分区策略，利用两者在内存和通信上的不对称性，相比TRL库实现了最高67%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KD框架（如TRL）对称地处理教师和学生模型，忽视了它们在内存占用和通信需求上的显著不对称性，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出HPC-aware的KD方法，解耦教师和学生模型的分区策略；结合垂直与水平模型分区，推导出解析表达式以识别分区策略间的拐点；避免不必要的教师模型数据结构并选择最优拆分策略。

Result: 相比TRL，每秒处理样本数提升最高达67%；在公司的生产HPC集群上显著加速了GKD训练。

Conclusion: 利用教师-学生不对称性并通过拓扑感知的并行化策略，能够显著提升知识蒸馏训练在HPC集群上的效率。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) enables training smaller student models under the guidance of larger teacher models, and the widely adopted TRL library implements it. Yet, TRL treats both models symmetrically, missing opportunities to exploit their pronounced asymmetry in memory footprint, and communication requirements. This paper presents an HPC-aware methodology for KD that decouples teacher and student partitioning efficiently. Our approach achieves up to 67% higher samples-per-second than TRL by avoiding unnecessary teacher-model data structures and selecting the best split strategy. We combine vertical and horizontal partitioning of models, deriving an analytical expression that identifies the existence of inflection points between splitting regimes. These results showed that exploiting teacher--student asymmetry through topology-aware parallelism notably accelerated GKD training on production HPC clusters at our company

</details>


### [67] [RAMSES: Secure high-performance computing for sensitive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27919)
*Peter Heger,Lech Nieroda,Roland Pabel,Christoph Stollwerk,Stefan Borowski,Kamil Tokmakov,Michael Commer,Martin Peifer,Stefan Wesner,Viktor Achter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RAMSES是一个从零开始设计的高性能计算系统，通过集成硬件内存加密、文件加密和OS强化等安全措施，在保证高性能的同时满足GDPR、ISO 27001和FIPS等数据保护标准，并在生物医学应用中验证了其安全开销有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC系统追求速度，安全系统牺牲速度换取安全，而生命科学等科学领域需要同时具备高性能和安全性以处理大规模敏感数据。

Method: 集成AMD处理器硬件内存加密、IBM Storage Scale和Thales CipherTrust管理器的文件加密，实现数据全生命周期加密（静态、传输、使用中）；实施OS强化、多层安全架构和强制多因素认证。

Result: 生物医学领域基准测试表明，安全环境对性能的影响有限，证明了速度与安全性这对矛盾需求可以共存。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的架构，可以在HPC系统中同时实现高性能和强安全性，构建一个连贯、灵活且用户友好的安全计算平台。

Abstract: Traditionally, the architecture of high-performance computing (HPC) systems is tailored for speed, while highly secure computer systems must sacrifice speed for security. However, a wide range of scientific domains, such as the life sciences, call for a combination of performance and security to allow processing sensitive data at scale. Here, we present RAMSES (Research Accelerator for Modeling and Simulation with Enhanced Security), an HPC system designed from the ground up to deliver high performance within a robust security framework. RAMSES integrates hardware-based memory encryption of AMD processors with state-of-the-art file encryption from IBM Storage Scale and the Thales CipherTrust manager, establishing an HPC platform that ensures continuous encryption throughout the data life cycle - at rest, in transit, and in use - in compliance with major data protection standards (European General Data Protection Regulation, ISO/IEC 27001 certification, and Federal Information Processing Standards). In addition, we implemented advanced operating system hardening, a multi-layered security architecture, and mandatory multi-factor authentication to adapt the HPC environment to increased security demands. Benchmark results from the biomedical sector demonstrate that the performance impact of the secure environment is limited and that integration of the conflicting requirements speed and security can be achieved while preserving a coherent, flexible, and user-friendly system.

</details>


### [68] [DataStates-LLM: Scalable Checkpointing for Transformer Models Using Composable State Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16956)
*Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DataStates-LLM 是一种新型检查点架构，通过状态提供者（State Providers）解耦状态抽象与数据移动，利用模型参数在前后向传播中的不变性实现惰性、非阻塞的异步快照，有效解决大规模 LLM 训练中检查点的异构性和序列化瓶颈，在 256 块 A100 GPU 上最高提升 4 倍检查点吞吐量，端到端训练时间减少 2.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检查点方案将模型状态视为不透明二进制数据，忽略了内存位置（GPU vs 主机）、逻辑对象分片、数据类型（张量 vs Python 对象）及其序列化需求的"3D 异构性"，导致阻塞的设备到主机传输、数据无关的序列化和存储 I/O 争用等严重运行时开销。

Method: 提出 DataStates-LLM 架构，核心引入 State Providers 解耦状态抽象与数据移动；利用模型参数在前后向传播中的不可变性，实现惰性非阻塞异步快照；通过 State Providers 高效合并碎片化的异构分片，并将元数据序列化与批量张量 I/O 重叠执行。

Result: 在 256 块 A100-40GB GPU 上对 70B 参数模型进行实验，相比现有最优方案，检查点吞吐量提升最高 4 倍，端到端训练时间减少最高 2.2 倍。

Conclusion: DataStates-LLM 有效缓解了超大规模 LLM 训练中的序列化和异构性瓶颈，为大规模分布式模型检查点提供了高效的新范式。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Large Transformer-based models, specifically Large Language Models (LLMs), now scaling to trillions of parameters, has necessitated training across thousands of GPUs using complex hybrid parallelism strategies (e.g., data, tensor, and pipeline parallelism). Checkpointing this massive, distributed state is critical for a wide range of use cases, such as resilience, suspend-resume, investigating undesirable training trajectories, and explaining model evolution. However, existing checkpointing solutions typically treat model state as opaque binary blobs, ignoring the ``3D heterogeneity'' of the underlying data structures--varying by memory location (GPU vs. Host), number of ``logical'' objects sharded and split across multiple files, data types (tensors vs. Python objects), and their serialization requirements. This results in significant runtime overheads due to blocking device-to-host transfers, data-oblivious serialization, and storage I/O contention. In this paper, we introduce DataStates-LLM, a novel checkpointing architecture that leverages State Providers to decouple state abstraction from data movement. DataStates-LLM exploits the immutability of model parameters during the forward and backward passes to perform ``lazy'', non-blocking asynchronous snapshots. By introducing State Providers, we efficiently coalesce fragmented, heterogeneous shards and overlap the serialization of metadata with bulk tensor I/O. We evaluate DataStates-LLM on models up to 70B parameters on 256 A100-40GB GPUs. Our results demonstrate that DataStates-LLM achieves up to 4$\times$ higher checkpointing throughput and reduces end-to-end training time by up to 2.2$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art solutions, effectively mitigating the serialization and heterogeneity bottlenecks in extreme-scale LLM training.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OverFlowLight是一个实时交通信号控制框架，通过多模态感知检测并动态插入专用溢流相位来预防止溢流，在三个城市43个交叉口的实际部署中减少60.4%的溢流事件并提升18.2%的网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法主要优化吞吐量，未能有效解决高峰期的队列溢流问题，导致级联拥堵和安全隐患。

Method: 提出OverFlowLight框架：(1)利用摄像头和雷达的多模态感知实时检测溢流；(2)动态生成并插入专用溢流相位清除阻塞队列；(3)混合控制设计结合基于规则的快速溢流干预和强化学习等后端控制器以优化长期效率。

Result: 在三城市43个交叉口部署，相比基线减少60.4%溢流事件，提升18.2%网络吞吐量，显著降低人工干预需求，且能与现有RL控制器无缝集成。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个实用、可扩展、数据驱动的主动防交通拥堵框架，为建设韧性高效的城市交通系统提供关键组件。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [70] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的RANSAC评分函数，通过共轭逆伽马先验对内点尺度进行解析边缘化，完全消除了对用户设定残差阈值的依赖，在近7万图像对基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RANSAC变体需要用户提供依赖于内点尺度的残差阈值参数，而该尺度本身需从受污染数据中估计，形成循环依赖。本文旨在消除这一依赖，通过逆转常规推理顺序实现无尺度参数的评分。

Method: 在固定内点划分下，利用共轭逆伽马先验对内点尺度进行解析闭式边缘化，推导出统一的闭式评分表达式，涵盖非信息性Jeffreys极限和经验贝叶斯先验；通过排序扫描实现O(N log N)计算；评分函数中完全不包含内点尺度参数。

Result: 在近7万图像对的两视图估计基准上（涵盖人工和学习的特征管线），所提评分在阈值误标定时精度几乎不下降（基线方法显著退化），仅需2个验证对即可达到近最优精度（基线方法需约100倍数据），且随着验证数据减少能收紧先验正则化。

Conclusion: 所提评分是首个公式中真正不包含内点尺度的RANSAC评分，在不同数据丰裕度下自适应，显著优于RANSAC、MSAC、GaU、MAGSAC等现有方法。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>


### [71] [Unified Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting: A Darts Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27438)
*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts库新增统一FoundationModel接口，无缝集成Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM等基础模型，实现零样本/微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，用户仅需更改模型名称即可使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通常以独立包发布，接口碎片化且与常用工具互操作性差，导致联合评估和流水线集成困难。

Method: 在Darts中开发统一的FoundationModel类集合，提供标准化全周期预测接口，最小化外部依赖，将基础模型集成到生态系统。

Result: 现有Darts流水线仅需更改模型名称即可使用基础模型；新流水线可用于零样本/微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，结合数据处理和评估工具。

Conclusion: Darts通过统一接口成功将基础模型融入时间序列分析流水线，降低了使用门槛，促进了从训练定制模型到利用预训练通用预测器的范式转变。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>


### [72] [PairSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability from Pair Representations in Protein Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27440)
*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PairSAE，通过N-mode SVD压缩成对张量为token级交互角色，再用稀疏自编码器学习共享的token级特征，实现对结构生物学基础模型（特别是pairformer架构）的可解释性分析，避免了传统SAE在成对表示上的二次膨胀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在transformer风格的序列嵌入上有效，但无法直接迁移到pairformer类架构：直接操作成对表示会产生特征数量的二次爆炸，且难以捕获序列和成对表示中联合分布的概念。

Method: PairSAE首先通过N-mode SVD将成对张量（pairwise tensors）总结为token级的交互角色，然后使用稀疏自编码器学习一组共享的token级特征，这些特征可解码为序列表示和成对表示。

Result: 在Boltz-2模型对PLINDER蛋白-配体复合物的激活值上评估，PairSAE生成了与UniProt注释一致的可解释特征，并能预测Boltz-2的亲和力值。

Conclusion: PairSAE将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念联系起来，阐明了模型"知道"什么，同时避免了传统SAE在pairformer架构中遇到的局限。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [73] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOGGYTRUST 是 FLTrust 的分层扩展，通过将信任计算本地化到雾节点，在保持鲁棒性的同时处理全局异构数据，在 CIFAR-10 上相比 FLTrust 提升超 50%，并在真实野生动物监测数据集中验证了有效性。
