<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Byzantine 鲁棒联邦学习方法（如 FLTrust）在全局数据异构场景下表现受限，难以同时处理信任估计中的分布偏移和不同客户端组间的漂移问题。

Method: 提出分层架构 FOGGYTRUST，将信任计算下放到雾节点（fog nodes），实现局部同质组内的鲁棒聚合；并结合局部信任聚合与异构感知全局优化器（如 FedAdam 和 SCAFFOLD）来同时解决分布偏移和客户端漂移。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 上针对 Krum 和 Trim 攻击相比 FLTrust 提升超过 50%；在真实野生动物监测数据集上也验证了分层信任网络的有效性。

Conclusion: FOGGYTRUST 通过分层信任架构有效提升了异构环境下的鲁棒联邦学习性能，在安全关键的社会影响力场景（如分布式野生动物监测）中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [74] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫强盗问题中状态不可观测且决策时点可能受限下的遗憾最小化，提出自退化马尔可夫强盗模型，证明了无先验知识时切换臂次数少的算法遗憾必为超对数阶，并设计了近乎对数遗憾的UCB-NOM算法，同时展示了先验知识可实现对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有马尔可夫强盗研究通常假设状态可观测或允许任意切换策略，但实际应用中状态常不可观测且切换成本高。作者希望研究在状态不可观测且决策时点受限的条件下，学习算法与最优纯策略之间的遗憾下界与上界。

Method: 1) 提出自退化马尔可夫强盗模型，推广了经典休息马尔可夫强盗；2) 构建遗憾下界证明，说明无先验知识时罕见切换策略的遗憾必为ω(log T)；3) 设计基于乐观思想的UCB-NOM算法；4) 引入偏差函数界作为先验知识，实现对数遗憾与O(√(T log T))最坏情况遗憾界。

Result: 1) 无先验知识时，罕见切换算法的遗憾下界为ω(log T)；2) UCB-NOM算法实现近乎对数遗憾；3) 已知偏差函数界时，UCB-NOM达到O(log T)遗憾和O(√(T log T))最坏情况遗憾；4) 遗憾界不依赖马尔可夫链的状态数。

Conclusion: 在自退化马尔可夫强盗中，状态不可观测性仅带来轻微不便——虽无法达到严格对数遗憾，但通过乐观算法仍可接近对数遗憾；先验知识可进一步弥合差距。该结论表明状态不可观测并非该模型下的根本性困难。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [75] [Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28217)
*Young Yoon,Jimin Kim,Soyeon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在完全委托的AI合作社中基于价值条件进行奖励分配的框架，利用遍历学习（TL）实现细粒度贡献归因和价值对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习（如FedAvg）在贡献归因上粒度不足，难以在异构价值约束下对数据提供者进行公平奖励分配，且缺乏价值对齐机制。

Method: 构建了价值条件梯度过滤、在线边际贡献信号和累积收益结算机制，基于遍历学习（TL）实现去中心化反向传播，保留显式遍历和梯度路径以实现更精细的归因。

Result: TL相比聚合中心化分布式学习和FedAvg式联邦学习，在归因粒度上更优，且无质量损失。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习和多元对齐提供了新的理论支撑和实践路径。

Abstract: We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [76] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prism Transformer通过渐进式增加每层注意力头数量的方式，替代了传统Transformer中所有层均分隐藏维度的均匀头配置，在不增加参数和计算量的前提下，建立了从局部到全局的表征层次结构，在多个模型规模上一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多头注意力机制在所有层均匀分配隐藏维度，导致早期层头数过多、每个头的子空间维度受限，无法有效捕获复杂的高维上下文模式，这构成了结构性的瓶颈。

Method: 提出Prism Transformer，采用渐进式头调度（progressive head schedule），即随层数增加单调递增注意力头数量：早期层使用少量但宽的头捕获复杂局部组合模式，深层层使用大量但窄的头将模式分解为专门的语言特征。该方法参数中立、计算中立，零训练推理开销。

Result: 在124M、354M和757M三种模型规模上，Prism Transformer一致优于均匀基线，验证损失持续降低，在PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy、WinoGrande等零样本基准上持续提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀的子空间分配能够在标准Transformer预算内释放潜在容量，使模型容量得到更有效的利用。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [77] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对二维平环上不同长宽比的三次非线性薛定谔方程，提出了一种几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO），以长宽比参数ω²作为额外输入，成功捕捉了有理数与无理数环面上不同的高频级联行为和Sobolev范数演化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维平环的长宽比决定了傅里叶共振结构，有理数与无理数几何展现出不同的高频级联行为。现有数值方法难以高效学习这种几何依赖的非线性色散偏微分方程的谱转移现象，因此需要一种几何感知的神经算子来捕捉不同几何下的解动力学差异。

Method: 提出几何条件化傅里叶神经算子（FNO），输入包括解的实部与虚部以及长宽比参数ω²。模型训练用于近似单步解算子，采用傅里叶伪谱方法生成随机相位初始数据的轨迹进行评估。通过消融实验研究保留傅里叶模态、激活函数、傅里叶层深度和显式几何条件化的作用。

Result: 学习得到的算子能捕捉两种环面上的主要解动力学，再现有理数环面上更强的H²增长和无理数环面上更受限的H²行为，与文献结果一致。包含ω²显着提高了长时间预测精度，尤其在有理数几何情形下。

Conclusion: 几何条件化的神经算子能有效学习非线性色散PDE中的谱转移现象，显式加入几何参数（如ω²）对于提高模型在具有不同共振结构的几何上的泛化能力至关重要，为几何感知的算子学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [78] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补的估计量，发现自然间接效应（NIE）不仅包含特定组件的因果效应，还包含交互效应（INT）。INT无法通过调整估计器或分析单元消除，其大小随干净与修补激活之间的距离缩放，在局部仿射模型中可忽略，并可分解为成对及高阶群组交互。INT不是待消除的干扰，而是可诊断因果结论是否依赖提示、以及贪婪NIE排序是否会遗漏组合搜索发现机制的重要指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补是机制可解释性的核心工具，但其估计量（NIE）是否纯粹反映特定组件的因果效应尚不明确。本文旨在从因果中介分析角度重新审视NIE，揭示其可能混杂的交互效应，并探讨这些交互效应的性质、可消除性及其对可解释性研究的意义。

Method: 从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补的估计量，识别出NIE中包含交互效应（INT）；通过理论证明INT的缩放性质、局部仿射条件下的可忽略性及其组合分解；在GPT-2 IOI电路上实验验证INT导致的失败模式（组件因果重要性被隐藏或膨胀，INT方差解释忠诚度分数的不稳定性）。

Result: NIE中的INT无法通过调整估计器或分析单元消除；INT随干净与修补激活之间的距离缩放；在局部仿射模型中INT可忽略；INT可分解为成对及高阶群组交互；在GPT-2 IOI电路中，条件相依的组件要么不可见要么被虚假膨胀，INT方差解释了忠诚度分数的不稳定性。

Conclusion: INT不是待消除的干扰，而是可诊断因果结论是否依赖提示、以及贪婪NIE排序是否会遗漏组合搜索发现机制的重要指标。INT的个体和群体级大小及符号可指示提示依赖性和机制可发现性。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>


### [79] [Boundary condition fidelity for bottom-hole pressure and CO2 plume prediction in geological carbon storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27515)
*Romal Ramadhan,Seyyed A. Hosseini,Larry W. Lake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了十种截断域边界处理方法对CO2地质封存中井底压力（BHP）和CO2羽流预测精度的影响，发现在均质和非均质储层中，保留角点孔隙体积是最关键因素；均匀处理会产生较大误差（BHP RMSE达250-382 psi，羽流IoU约0.80-0.84），而角点校正方案可将IoU提升至0.94以上；带有传导率校正的渐进修饰器表现最优，BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU超过0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质碳封存模拟中常使用截断域以减少计算量，但人工边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO2饱和度分布，导致预测失真。现有研究缺乏对不同边界处理方法的系统性比较，需要明确哪种边界处理方法能够最准确地还原全域参考解。

Method: 在均质和非均质储层中，将十种截断域边界处理方法（包括均匀孔隙体积乘子、传导率修饰器、角点修正孔隙体积校正、层状校正和渐进修饰器等）与全域参考模拟进行对比，使用BHP RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和羽流交并比（IoU）作为性能指标。

Result: 均匀处理方法忽略角点存储，BHP RMSE高达362-382 psi（均质）和250-304 psi（非均质），羽流IoU仅0.80-0.84（16-20%区域被错误表征）；角点校正方案可将压力误差大幅降低并将IoU提升至0.94以上；传导率校正并非普遍有益——均质储层中改善压力精度，非均质储层中过度限制流动反而增加误差；带有传导率校正的渐进修饰器在两类储层中均表现最优（BHP NRMSE<3.7%，IoU>0.97）。

Conclusion: 截断域模拟中保留角点孔隙体积是最关键的要求；均匀边界处理方法不可靠；传导率校正需要根据储层非均质性谨慎使用；渐进修饰器结合传导率校正是一种鲁棒的通用边界处理方法。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>


### [80] [Productionized Fairness Measurement Under Privacy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27558)
*Osonde A. Osoba,Yuzi He,Saikrishna Badrinarayanan,Varun Mithal,Sakshi Jain,Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPRE方法，通过结合隐私技术（安全两方计算、差分隐私、加法同态加密）与种族/族裔人口统计信号源（BISG估计器和稀疏黄金调查集），以隐私保护方式对美国LinkedIn成员进行种族/族裔相关的公平性测量，并展示了其在候选人和查看者侧公平性测量中的应用及可迁移框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种族/族裔等人口统计信号在法律上受限且文化敏感，难以用于公平性评估的分组分析，亟需一种隐私保护的方法来实现基于种族/族裔的公平性测量。

Method: PPRE方法在两种种族/族裔人口统计信号源（BISG估计器和稀疏自报人口统计调查集）之上，叠加应用安全两方计算、差分隐私和加法同态加密三种隐私技术，构建隐私保护的公平性测量解决方案。

Result: 详细阐述了PPRE的隐私保证，并展示了其在候选人侧和查看者侧公平性测量中的应用效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可供其他机构参考的可迁移框架，用于实施类似的隐私保护测量基础设施。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum Generative Diffusion Model for Real-World Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27561)
*Jack Waller,Filippo Caruso,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan,Xing Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个量子生成扩散模型QDiffusion-TS用于时间序列合成，在IQM量子处理器上验证。通过用量子神经网络替换经典扩散Transformer中的前馈组件，参数减少近三个数量级。在金融时间序列上，生成数据更接近真实分布（Wasserstein距离降低44%），下游预测任务中RMSE提升高达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典生成模型随规模扩大面临计算成本和效率挑战，量子机器学习提供了一种使用紧凑高表达力模型表示复杂数据分布的有前景替代方案。

Method: 提出QDiffusion-TS，首个量子生成扩散模型，将经典扩散架构中的去噪Transformer前馈组件替换为量子神经网络，形成混合量子Transformer架构。

Result: 在Apple和Amazon金融时间序列上，Wasserstein距离较经典模型降低约44%；下游预测任务中，数据增强使RMSE提升高达71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能以更少参数匹配并常超越经典性能，为更高效可扩展的数据驱动生成建模建立了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>


### [82] [hia-gat: A Heterogeneous Interaction-Aware Graph Attention Network For Frame-Level Traffic Conflict Risk Prediction On Freeways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27577)
*Mahshid Malazizi,Seyedmehdi Khaleghian,Mina Sartipi,Toru Hirano,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HIA-GAT，一种用于高速公路逐帧风险评估的双流异构图注意力网络，通过纵向和横向交互的专用注意力路径及冲突类型感知门控机制，在NGSIM数据集上实现了最优的风险排名性能（AUC 0.835/0.867），并提供可解释的逐车辆冲突类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将高速公路逐帧风险评估形式化为多智能体场景图级别的二分类问题，解决现有方法对纵向（同车道）和横向（邻车道）交互关系差异化建模不足的问题，特别是横向冲突风险对图结构信息的依赖。

Method: 构建每帧的关系感知图，以车辆为节点、同车道（纵向）和邻车道（横向）为两种边类型，并增强物理信息边特征；提出HIA-GAT，一种双流异构图注意力网络，通过专用注意力路径处理纵向和横向交互，并通过冲突类型感知门控机制融合，门控受SSM冲突归因的事件级监督。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集上，九种TTC和PET阈值配置下，HIA-GAT达到最佳平均风险排名性能（I-80上AUC 0.835，US-101上AUC 0.867），在仅使用PET（换道）设置中增益最大；图结构对横向冲突风险建模至关重要，纵向风险可通过非关系聚合捕获。

Conclusion: HIA-GAT在高速公路风险评估中优于非图模型和图基线方法，学习到的门控机制提供可解释的逐车辆主导冲突类型归因，支持可操作的实时高速公路安全监控。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [83] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS是一种基于经验贝叶斯收缩的逐标注者仿射校准方法，用于RLHF奖励模型中的标注者偏差校正，无需重新训练模型即可在PRISM和PluriHarms数据集上分别实现8.58%和9.66%的RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF奖励模型将所有标注者的偏好数据混合池化，拟合单一的全局仿射校准器，忽略了标注者之间系统性的评分尺度偏移和斜率差异，导致聚合后的平均拟合无法代表任何单个标注者的真实偏好。

Method: PEBS首先为每位标注者在其标注数据的保留子集上拟合独立的仿射校准器，然后应用Morris-James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩方法，将这些逐标注者估计向总体均值进行收缩，整个过程在闭式解中完成，无需重新训练奖励模型。

Result: 在PRISM数据集上，PEBS相比池化总体斜率基线将用户内保留集RMSE降低了8.58%；在PluriHarms harm评分（Qwen-2.5基座模型，同族数据）上实现了9.66%的RMSE降低。

Conclusion: PEBS是一种闭式后验估计方法，专用于RLHF奖励建模中的标注者特定仿射校准；它保持奖励基座模型不变，仅估计推理时用于新评分的标注者级映射，有效提升了偏好建模的个体准确性。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [84] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLHF中奖励信号异步问题，提出回溯优势校正（RAC），通过队列、非负核老化、裁剪残差注入实现延迟奖励下的无偏优势估计，在表格MDP上最多降低47.9倍策略偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境下的RLHF中，代码执行验证器、慢速评委集成或排队人工审核等异步奖励信号破坏了标准PPO的同步奖励假设，导致策略梯度估计有偏，亟需一种能处理延迟奖励的校正方法。

Method: 将每个未完成的慢速完成项排入队列，通过非负核（有效延迟核）进行老化处理，然后作为裁剪后的残差注入到下一优化步骤的优势函数中。核心在于利用无偏裁剪重要性比率，使得当有效延迟核注入全部质量时，累积RAC校正恰好无偏；否则偏差与未注入部分呈线性关系。在无延迟身份核下退化为V-trace。

Result: 在表格MDP概念验证中，双慢通道配置下RAC将闭环策略偏差最多降低47.9倍，且比等待慢速通道具有更低的挂钟成本。通过两行奖励管理器补丁即可集成到PPO和GRPO中。

Conclusion: RAC为异步奖励场景下的RLHF提供了理论保证且实用的解决方案，兼顾偏差控制与计算效率，易于集成到现有框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [85] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KARMA是一种通过构建马尔可夫替代模型来解释时间序列预测器的方法，能够捕捉模型学习到的时间依赖性，并提供五级全局解释层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法大多假设时间戳特征独立，忽略了时间序列数据的时间依赖性，导致解释可能违反数据的序列和因果结构。

Method: KARMA包含三个核心步骤：(1) 识别预测所需的最小历史长度K；(2) 从离散化的历史状态空间估计最优的K阶马尔可夫转移核；(3) 从马尔可夫转移核中推导出五级全局解释层次结构。

Result: 在真实天气数据（北京PM2.5）和复杂合成数据上的实验表明，KARMA能够恢复模型学习到的数据因果结构，并且在识别时间依赖性方面优于TimeSHAP等现有归因方法。

Conclusion: KARMA通过马尔可夫替代模型有效地解释了时间序列预测器，尊重了数据的时间依赖性和因果结构，在性能上超越了传统归因方法。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [86] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺乏直接通信条件下，利用智能体动作信息进行状态估计，并通过强化学习设计易于估计的策略（策略可观测性），在飞机追踪问题中实现了对任务性能影响最小的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理或操作约束常常导致自主智能体之间的通信受限，这给监控或多智能体协调带来了困难。即使没有强通信，仍有一些信息可用，但剩余的相关智能体状态需要通过估计来重建。利用动作作为信息源进行状态估计，并使其更易处理，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 采用强化学习训练策略，通过奖励函数鼓励策略的可观测性（Policy Observability），使得从动作中估计状态更加容易。训练后通过仿真分析所训练策略的可观测性。

Result: 在飞机追踪问题中，成功找到了一个具有增强可观测性的策略，且该策略对名义任务性能的影响极小。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习可以设计出既保持任务性能又具有良好可观测性的策略，为通信受限环境下的智能体状态估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [87] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COOPA是一个模块化LLM智能体架构，通过迭代置信度建模、元素级来源解释和多种求解器路由，在八个大模型骨架和四个基线中，六个骨架上的宏平均准确率最优，相比最强基线提升高达6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学建模需要深厚的领域知识、数学抽象和求解器专业知识，现有基于LLM的系统在复杂问题上准确率低、输出不透明且仅支持少数求解器，亟需一种可解释且可扩展的OR决策支持方案。

Method: 提出COOPA（合作运筹智能体），包含三个组件：1）迭代置信度建模——生成多个候选公式化，沿建模维度自评估，用最大-最小置信度准则选择最优方案；2）元素级来源与置信度解释——将变量、参数、约束、目标函数与引用源文本关联，提供人工验证的审计轨迹；3）多求解器路由——将问题路由至不同OR问题类的专用优化智能体。

Result: 在三个OR基准、八个LLM骨架和四个基线条件下，COOPA在六个骨架上的宏平均准确率最优，相比最强基线提升高达6.7个百分点。消融实验证实了迭代置信度建模的贡献，案例研究展示了来源可追溯性和多求解器分发的价值。

Conclusion: COOPA通过模块化设计、置信度驱动的迭代建模、可追溯的解释机制和多样化求解器支持，有效提升了LLM在运筹学决策支持中的准确性、可解释性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [88] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合离散-连续音频表示方法，结合时间压缩离散令牌和降维连续残差，通过混合编解码器和Transformer架构，在减少自回归步数的同时提升说话人特征保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散音频表示在多模态系统和LLM集成中日益重要，但离散化导致信息损失，造成下游任务性能下降。现有离散化方法在保留说话人特征等细粒度信息方面存在明显不足。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续焦斑调制编解码器（hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec）和混合Transformer架构。该框架在离散域进行自回归推理，同时结合非自回归预测和连续残差上采样。

Result: 相比纯离散方法，该方法在保留说话人特征方面有显著提升，同时减少了所需的自回归步数。

Conclusion: 混合离散-连续表示策略能有效缓解离散化带来的信息损失问题，在保持离散表示优势的同时提升了音频表示的保真度，为多模态音频-文本系统提供了更优的表示方案。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [89] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型语言模型在边缘设备上进行顺序LoRA个性化微调时的灾难性遗忘问题，提出通过检查点协议监控任务性能、遗忘情况和参考集漂移，并发现轻量级参考集分布诊断可以揭示模型特定的不稳定性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs在边缘设备上的个性化部署需要持续适应新数据，但顺序微调可能导致灾难性遗忘，损害模型在先前任务和通用能力上的表现。现有基准（如TRACE）已显示持续微调会显著降低大型对齐模型的通用能力，因此需要研究SLMs在持续学习场景下的稳定性监控方法。

Method: 采用顺序LoRA个性化微调方案，在每次适应阶段后保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、先前任务和固定参考集上评估。通过轻量级参考集分布诊断来监测模型特定的不稳定性模式，并与任务级指标进行对比分析。

Result: 轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示顺序LoRA个性化过程中模型特定的不稳定性模式，包括任务级指标无法察觉的有害适应情况。

Conclusion: 参考集分布诊断可以作为监控SLMs在持续学习场景中稳定性的有效工具，为未来的研究方向提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time. We show that lightweight reference set distributional diagnostics can reveal model-specific instability patterns during sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs, including cases where task-level metrics alone hide harmful adaptation. We hope this can highlight new research avenues for monitoring stability of SLMs in a continual learning setting.

</details>


### [90] [TeRoR: Decoupled Temporal Rotation with Relational Circular Region for Temporal Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27651)
*Peijia Xie,Yike Liu,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对TeRo模型在关系映射属性建模和时间信息表达上的局限性，本文提出TeRoR方法，通过在复数空间中对头尾实体进行独立旋转变换以增强时序建模能力，并引入半径约束机制来捕获关系的多种映射属性。实验表明TeRoR在四个TKG数据集上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeRo模型在处理关系的一对多、多对一、多对多等映射属性时表现不佳，且时间信息表达能力有限。

Method: 提出TeRoR方法：1) 将实体嵌入的时间演化进行划分，在复数向量空间中对头尾实体进行独立的旋转变换以增强时序信息建模能力；2) 训练一个半径，将旋转平移后的头实体约束在以尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，从而有效捕获关系的多种映射属性。

Result: 在四个不同的时序知识图谱数据集上，TeRoR相比现有最先进模型取得了具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: TeRoR通过独立旋转变换和半径约束机制，有效解决了TeRo在关系映射属性和时间信息表达上的不足，是一种简单高效的时序知识图谱嵌入方法。

Abstract: In recent years, with the emergence of Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs), research on learning entity and relation representations in TKGs has attracted increasing attention, giving rise to a large number of TKG embedding methods. TeRo is a simple and efficient temporal knowledge graph embedding approach. However, TeRo does not do well in modeling the mapping properties of various relations, such as one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many. Meanwhile, it also has limitations in the expression of temporal information. To address these issues, we propose a novel TKG embedding method named TeRoR. This method divides the temporal evolution of entity embeddings, and conducts independent rotation transformations on head and tail entities in the complex vector space to strengthen temporal information modeling capacity. In terms of relational characteristics, we train a radius to constrain the rotated and translated head entities within a circular region centered on the tail entity, which effectively captures the diverse mapping properties of relations. Experimental results demonstrate that TeRoR achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models on four distinct TKG datasets.

</details>


### [91] [Are Time-Series Foundation Models Ready for E-Nose Data? An Empirical Assessment of Their Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27672)
*Taeyeong Choi,Mohammed Kamruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在电子鼻（E-Nose）气体传感数据上的表现，发现微调是获得满意性能的必要条件，且将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合可进一步提升性能，揭示了当前TSFM在气体传感应用中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TSFMs在时间序列任务中展现了强大的泛化能力，但其在气体传感数据（特别是电子鼻数据）上的实用性尚未被探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 系统评估Chronos-2、MOMENT等代表性TSFMs在电子鼻数据上的气体识别与浓度预测任务，对比微调前后的表现，并探索TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示的融合策略。

Result: 微调是TSFMs在电子鼻数据上取得满意性能的必要条件；将TSFM嵌入与专用预测模型表示融合可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中存在潜力但仍有局限，微调和模型融合是提升性能的有效手段。

Abstract: Inspired by advances in natural language processing and computer vision, "time-series foundation models" (TSFMs) have recently been introduced with the promise of strong generalization across diverse time-series tasks, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, as well as across domains such as healthcare, climate science, and manufacturing. However, their utility for gas-sensing data remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper systematically evaluates recent TSFMs on electronic nose (E-Nose) data. In particular, we investigate whether embeddings produced by representative TSFMs, including Chronos-2 and MOMENT, provide effective representations for gas identification and concentration prediction. Specifically, we show that fine-tuning is necessary to achieve satisfactory performance on E-Nose data, and fusing TSFM embeddings with representations learned by specialized predictive models can further improve the performance, suggesting both the potential and limitations of current TSFMs for gas-sensing applications.

</details>


### [92] [Textual Belief States for World Models: Identifiable Representation Learning Under Strict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27681)
*Xiang Gao,Kaiwen Dong,Yuguang Yao,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Kamalika Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出严格潜在状态中介方法，通过因子化GRPO（fGRPO）在文本环境中强制使用离散、可解释、可变长度的文本潜在状态，解决LLM架构中因历史旁路导致的表示质量问题，实验显示在表示质量和 rollout 性能上分别提升高达57%和98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，基于LLM的世界模型存在历史旁路问题，使得潜在表示质量无法通过预测性能反映，潜在状态不可识别。严格潜在状态中介是解决此问题的经典原则，但在文本环境中因离散、不可微的潜在状态和强大的LLM解码器而难以实施。

Method: 提出文本潜在状态（离散、可解释、变长）和因子化GRPO（fGRPO），一种树结构强化学习方法，在训练过程中强制实施严格中介，使预测仅依赖于潜在状态和动作。

Result: 在TextWorld和ScienceWorld基准上，保持一步预测精度的同时，表示质量提升高达57%，rollout性能提升98%，且增益随任务复杂度和时间跨度增加而增大。

Conclusion: 严格潜在状态中介在文本领域是可行且有效的，能够使表示质量成为可经验检验的指标，显著优于历史泄漏架构。

Abstract: World models in partially observed environments rely on latent representations that summarize interaction history, but in many modern LLM-based architectures predictive performance fails to reflect representation quality due to history bypass, rendering the latent state unidentifiable. Strict latent state mediation, requiring predictions to depend only on the latent state and action, is a classical principle that resolves this, but enforcing it in text-based settings is an open challenge: textual latent states are discrete and non-differentiable, precluding variational training, and expressive LLM decoders readily ignore the bottleneck. We show how to make strict mediation work in the text domain. We formalize why it is necessary, showing that strict mediation makes representation quality empirically testable while history-leaky architectures break this connection. We then introduce textual latent states, which are discrete, interpretable, and variable-length, and factorized GRPO (fGRPO), a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that enforces strict mediation during training. Experiments on TextWorld and ScienceWorld show preserved one-step prediction accuracy alongside up to 57\% gains in representation quality and 98\% improvements in rollout performance, increasing with task complexity and horizon.

</details>


### [93] [CBD: API-Only LLM Black-Box Unlearning through Controlled Behavioral Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27683)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Yijing Lin,Zhipeng Gao,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CBD框架，一种仅需API黑盒访问的机器去学习框架，通过受控行为发散和梯度统计判别基，在目标数据与保留数据高度相似场景下实现有效的知识移除，在ToFU和WMDP基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备通过API调用大语言模型时，边缘生成的数据可能包含敏感、版权、有害或过时信息，需要机器去学习来移除这些数据的影响。但现有方法面临两个挑战：1) 仅能通过API黑盒访问，无法获取模型参数和内部logits；2) 当目标去学习数据与保留数据具有高度相似的提示结构或语义模式时，难以保持保留数据的效用。

Method: CBD框架包含两个核心组件：1) 使用两个辅助模型在保留输入和目标输入之间创建受控行为发散，并将发散转换为去学习相关性分数，将与去学习相关的提示路由远离目标LLM；2) 为应对高相似度场景，通过估计经验Fisher矩阵并求解正则化广义特征值问题，构建基于梯度统计的判别基，引导去学习信号指向目标特定信息而非共享的提示结构。

Result: 在ToFU forget10上，CBD在遗忘集上接近重新训练的参考模型，同时将模型效用提升至74.90，比第二好的基线高出约15%。在WMDP上，将危险知识准确率降至25.68（接近随机猜测），同时保持MMLU准确率为52.67。

Conclusion: 与11种白盒和灰盒去学习基线相比，CBD实现了更好的去学习-效用权衡，且性能在不同设置下变化很小，证明了其作为仅需API黑盒访问的去学习框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Edge devices increasingly invoke large language models (LLMs) through API services for context aware edge intelligence, while edge generated data may be collected to improve LLMs and may introduce sensitive, copyrighted, harmful, or outdated information into model behavior. Machine unlearning offers a practical way to remove the influence of undesired data without retraining LLMs. However, existing methods still face two gaps. The first is API only black box access, where target model parameters and internal logits are unavailable. The second is how to preserve retained utility when unlearning target data and retained data share highly similar prompt structures or semantic patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Controlled Behavioral Divergence (CBD), an API only black box unlearning framework. CBD uses two auxiliary models to create controlled behavioral divergence between retained inputs and unlearning target inputs, converts this divergence into an unlearning relevance score, and routes unlearning related prompts away from the target LLM. To improve discrimination accuracy under high similarity between target and retained data, CBD constructs a gradient statistics based discriminative basis by estimating empirical Fisher matrices and solving a regularized generalized eigenvalue problem, guiding the unlearning signal toward target specific information rather than shared prompt structures. Compared with eleven white box and gray box unlearning baselines, CBD achieves a better unlearning utility trade off and its performance varies little across settings. On ToFU forget10, CBD approaches the retrained reference on the forget set while raising model utility to 74.90, about 15% above the second best baseline. On WMDP, it lowers hazardous knowledge accuracy to 25.68, near random guessing, while preserving MMLU accuracy of 52.67. Code is at https://github.com/DGL-codes/CBD.

</details>


### [94] [Deployment-Side Adaptiveness in Multi-Horizon Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27688)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在多期波动率预测中，训练好的多输出（MIMO）模型在推理时的不同部署策略（即滚动规则）会产生不同的预测精度和计算成本。通过实验发现，非默认滚动规则通常优于标准MIMO部署，但最佳规则因架构和预测期而异。基于验证集选择的单规则策略以低成本提升性能，而小规则子集能以较低推理成本达到接近集成方法的效果。此外，基于MSE选择的策略不能很好地迁移到QLIKE损失函数上，表明部署策略的选择对评估指标敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测中，模型性能不仅取决于训练好的模型本身，还取决于模型如何部署。现有研究多关注模型架构，但忽略了推理时的部署策略（如滚动规则）对预测效果的影响，尤其是在多期波动率预测中。

Method: 以训练好的多输出（MIMO）预测器为基础，通过改变推理时的滚动规则生成一族预测器。在20个股票波动率序列、三个预测期上，使用线性模型到PatchTST等多种架构进行实验。对比默认MIMO与非默认滚动规则，并评估基于验证集的部署选择策略（包括单规则和小规则子集）。

Result: 非默认滚动规则通常优于标准MIMO部署，但最佳固定规则因架构和预测期而异。验证集选择的单规则策略以低成本提升性能，小规则子集能以较低推理成本接近集成效果。但MSE选择的策略对QLIKE损失迁移性差。

Conclusion: 推理时的部署策略是金融预测中重要的适应性来源；评估训练好的波动率预测器不应仅关注架构，还应考虑其部署策略。

Abstract: In financial forecasting, predictive performance depends not only on which model is trained, but also on how the trained model is deployed. We study this issue in multi-horizon volatility forecasting. Our starting point is that a trained multi-output (MIMO) forecaster does not define a single deployable predictor: by changing the inference-time rollout rule, the same trained model induces a family of forecasts with different accuracy and cost profiles. Across 20 stock-volatility series, three forecast horizons, and architectures ranging from linear models to PatchTST, we find that non-default rollout rules often improve over standard MIMO deployment. However, the best fixed rule varies substantially across architectures and horizons, making any single static replacement unreliable. We therefore evaluate validation-based deployment policies over the induced rule family. Under the primary MSE objective, validation-selected singletons provide a low-cost improvement over default MIMO, while small rule subsets recover much of the benefit of larger ensembles at substantially lower inference cost. We also find that policy rankings are metric-sensitive: MSE-selected policies do not transfer uniformly to QLIKE, a finance-standard volatility loss. These results show that inference-time deployment is a meaningful source of adaptiveness in financial forecasting, and that trained volatility forecasters should be evaluated not only by their architecture, but also by their deployment policy.

</details>


### [95] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于元学习的任意有效认证鲁棒性框架，通过轻量级元学习器预测图像先验并结合序列E过程，在保持严格统计保证的同时实现20倍样本复杂度降低，并支持自适应计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）提供了严格的鲁棒性保证但计算成本极高，传统方法需要数万次模型评估且无法动态调整样本量，限制了其在实时安全关键场景中的部署。

Method: 采用轻量级元学习器预测图像特定的先验分布，结合序列E过程实现任意有效（anytime-valid）的统计推断，支持自适应样本量分配和计算资源调度。

Result: 相比传统方法实现20倍样本复杂度降低，在保持同等认证性能的同时，支持基于应用风险阈值的自适应计算分配。

Conclusion: 该方法为实时、安全关键的认证部署提供了可行路径，实现了传统认证框架无法完成的资源分诊（resource triage）。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) provides rigorous robustness guarantees for neural networks without architectural constraints, yet its adoption is limited by extreme computational costs. Standard RS requires tens of thousands of model evaluations per input and forces practitioners to commit to fixed sample sizes a priori. In this work, we present a novel meta-learning framework for anytime-valid certified robustness that adaptively deploys computational resources. By using a lightweight meta-learner to predict image-specific priors for a sequential E-process, we achieve a 20-fold reduction in sample complexity compared to traditional methods while maintaining rigorous statistical guarantees. Beyond raw efficiency, we demonstrate how anytime-validity enables adaptively allocating compute based upon application-specific risk thresholds, a form of resource triage impossible under classic certification frameworks. That this is achievable while also providing similar certification performance demonstrates that our approach provides a pathway for real-time, safety-critical certification deployments.

</details>


### [96] [Class-frequency Guided Noise Schedule for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27696)
*Jiequan Cui,Beier Zhu,Qingshan Xu,Xiaojuan Qi,Bei Yu,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了扩散模型中类别频率与多尺度噪声调度之间的相关性，发现低频类别因低密度区域更大导致分数估计更不准确，且高频类别主导分数空间，使得低频类别生成质量差、多样性有限。为此提出CFRG噪声调度方法，为低频类别分配更大尺度噪声，在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT等不平衡数据集上的图像生成、分类和文生图任务中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中低密度区域导致分数估计不准确，且现有多尺度噪声调度无法解决低频类别面临更大低密度区域的问题，导致低频类别生成质量差、多样性有限。

Method: 提出类别频率引导的噪声调度（CFRG），核心思想是为低频类别分配更大尺度的噪声，以缓解其低密度区域问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT和ImageNet-LT等不平衡数据集上，CFRG在图像生成、图像分类和文生图任务中均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 频率统计信息在噪声调度设计中起着关键作用，CFRG通过为低频类别分配更大噪声有效改善了生成质量和多样性。

Abstract: In this paper, we are the first to examine the correlations between class frequency and the multi-scale noise schedule within diffusion models. For score-based generative models, low-density regions often lead to inaccurately estimated scores, thereby compromising the generation quality. Although the multi-scale noise schedule can alleviate this issue during the diffusion process, low-frequency classes still face the challenge of large low-density regions, resulting in more inaccurate estimated scores than high-frequency classes. Furthermore, high-frequency classes tend to dominate the score space, causing a convergence of most data points towards generating samples from these classes. Consequently, samples generated within low-frequency classes exhibit suboptimal quality and limited diversity. To address this challenge, we propose the \textit{Class-frequency Guided (CFRG)} noise schedule, leveraging the insight that low-frequency classes should be endowed with larger-scale noises. To illustrate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct experiments on various tasks, including image generation, image classification, and text-to-image generation, using imbalanced datasets, \textit{i.e.}, CIFAR-100-LT, and ImageNet-LT. By employing the CFRG noise schedule, we achieve substantial improvements over baselines, manifesting the crucial role of frequency statistics in noise schedule design.

</details>


### [97] [What Was That Again? Certified Robustness for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27698)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Neil Marchant,Jiani Xie,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于认证机制的双门诊断流程（双面原子审计+排名锦标赛），在不依赖真实转录先验知识的情况下，显著降低ASR系统的词错误率（最高55%），提高召回率，并提供细粒度的词级和句级认证以增强声学安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统对对抗性和良性扰动高度敏感，但在部署系统中检测此类行为极其困难，因为缺乏真实转录的先验知识（oracle knowledge）。

Method: 提出双门诊断流程：(1) 双面原子审计，通过累积统计置信度来认证令牌存在性和对抗性排除；(2) 基于排名的锦标赛，用于选择获胜的转录序列。

Result: 在四种不同架构上评估，词错误率相对降低高达55%，同时提高了召回率，降低了置信度与WER之间的Spearman相关性。

Conclusion: 受认证启发的机制能够有效提升ASR系统的鲁棒性和安全性，提供词级和句级细粒度认证，增强声学安全。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition systems are notoriously both sensitive to adversarial and benign perturbations. While this has been repeatedly demonstrated using reference datasets, detecting such behaviors in deployed systems is incredibly challenging, due to the absence of oracle knowledge of the true transcription. We demonstrate that employing a certification-inspired mechanism can significantly decrease WER, increase recall, and decrease the Spearman correlation between confidence and WER. We achieve this through a dual-gate diagnostic pipeline: a Two-Sided Atomic Audit that accumulates statistical wealth to certify both token existence and adversarial exclusion, and a Rank-Based Tournament that selects the winning sequence. Our evaluations across four diverse architectures demonstrate up to a 55% relative reduction in Word Error Rate, while also providing granular word- and sentence-level certifications to enhance acoustic security.

</details>


### [98] [The Simulacrum: Decision-Theoretic Pretraining for Near-Optimal Time-Series Forecasting and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27711)
*Pablo Montero-Manso,Marcel Scharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络的决策理论预训练框架，通过在生成性世界上训练神经网络来近似最优决策规则，从而为时间序列分析提供零样本推理的神经估计器，在有限样本性质、偏差控制、极小化最优性和均匀校准方面超越传统方法，并在实际基准测试中达到竞争或最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列估计方法（如MLE、AICc）在处理有限样本偏差、校准不足以及分析上难以处理或计算上不可行的优化准则时存在局限性，且无法灵活地根据具体决策目标定制估计器。

Method: 框架包含三个核心要素：(1) 指定一个生成性世界（数据生成过程的分布）；(2) 定义目标决策目标（决策损失函数）；(3) 在分层模拟数据上训练神经网络，使其近似最优决策规则。训练好的网络可直接用于未见过的真实时间序列的零样本推理。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 神经估计器在相同模型结构下优于MLE和AICc等传统基准；(2) 即使在纯结构模型模拟上训练，在真实世界基准上也能达到与统计、神经或大型预训练模型竞争甚至最优的预测精度；(3) 成功解决了AR(p)模型的有限样本偏差/校准问题和预测组合难题。

Conclusion: 该框架的主要优势在于能够近似求解分析上棘手或计算上昂贵的时间序列问题，包括复杂结构方程或最优性准则。通过显式控制决策理论权衡，为分析人员提供高度定制化的高效估计工具，弥合了理论最优性与实际可计算性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We introduce a neural network-based framework for learning time series estimators through a process we term decision-theoretic pretraining. Analysts specify a generative world, a distribution over data-generating processes, and a target decision objective. A neural network trained on stratified simulations from this world approximates the corresponding optimal decision rule, yielding a neural estimator that provides forecasts, parameter estimates, predictive intervals, or model-selection for zero-shot inference on previously unseen time series.
  The joint specification of the generative world and objective enables the estimators to directly approximate process-level, finite-sample properties: near-optimal risk, bias control, minimax performance, and uniform calibration. Our experiments demonstrate that these neural estimators can outperform traditional baselines such as maximum likelihood estimation and model selection via AICc, for the same model structural model classes. Furthermore, even when trained purely on simulations of structural models, they achieve competitive or state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy on major real-world benchmarks, compared with statistical, neural or large pre-trained models.
  We illustrate the framework by addressing two longstanding challenges: finite-sample bias and miscalibration in AR(p) models, and the forecast combination puzzle. These applications highlight the approach's main advantage: its ability to approximate solutions to analytically intractable or computationally prohibitive time series problems, including complex structural equations or optimality criteria. Ultimately, by enabling explicit control over decision-theoretic trade-offs, the framework equips analysts with highly efficient estimation tools tailored to their specific analytical needs.

</details>


### [99] [Aurora: A Leverage-Aware Spectral Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27715)
*Alec Dewulf,Dhruv Pai,Li Yang,Ashley Zhang,Ben Keigwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Aurora优化器，通过在保持Muon极分解几何特性的同时强制参数行归一化，解决了Muon优化器在宽矩阵（如MLP投影层）中行范数不均匀导致的神经元退化问题。实验表明Aurora优于Muon，并在modded-nanoGPT速度赛中达到光谱优化器的最佳性能，且收益随MLP扩展因子增大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在处理宽矩阵参数（如MLP层投影矩阵）时，更新量的行范数存在任意非均匀性，导致某些神经元持续接收微小更新，逐渐丧失对网络输出的有效贡献，形成自我强化的反馈循环。

Method: 提出Aurora优化器，在保留Muon基于动量矩阵极分解的几何结构前提下，强制执行矩阵参数更新的行均匀性约束，通过额外的行归一化步骤解决行范数不均匀问题，同时避免偏离极分解几何。

Result: Aurora在预训练实验中优于Muon；与现有方法结合后，在modded-nanoGPT速度赛优化器赛道中达到光谱优化器的最佳性能；Aurora相对于Muon的改进幅度随MLP扩展因子增大而提升。

Conclusion: Aurora通过保持Muon极分解几何的同时强制行均匀性，有效解决了宽矩阵参数的行范数不均匀问题，可能支持非常宽的MLP层的高效训练。

Abstract: We show that for tall matrix parameters, like projection matrices in the MLP layers, the Muon update can have row norms that are arbitrarily non-uniform. This can lead to a self-reinforcing feedback loop whereby neurons receive persistently small updates and eventually do not contribute meaningfully to network outputs. This problem is effectively mitigated by an additional row normalization step, but current methods do this in a way that moves the Muon update geometry away from the polar factor of the momentum matrix, which we find is undesirable. We propose Aurora, an optimizer that enforces row-uniformity of matrix parameter updates while respecting Muon's polar factor geometry. Aurora outperforms Muon in our pre-training experiments and, when combined with existing methods, achieves state-of-the-art performance among spectral optimizers on the optimizer track of the modded-nanoGPT speedrun. Additionally, we find that Aurora's empirical gains over Muon scale with the MLP expansion factor, suggesting that Aurora may allow for effective training of very wide MLP layers.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum II: Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27721)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miroslav Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过半自动机学习问题，理论证明了基于自动课程学习的分治策略（将长序列递归分解为短子问题）相比直接求解，在监督微调场景下可将监督样本需求从线性Ω(T)降至次多项式2^{O(√log T)}，在强化学习场景下可将参考模型覆盖条件从完整序列长度T指数级削弱至短块长度B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解组合泛化的理论基础：何时以及为何将问题分解为子问题比直接求解能带来更高效的学习。

Method: 以半自动机模拟（状态跟踪、正则语言识别、模算术）为规范问题，采用基于自动课程学习的递归分解策略：将长序列递归分解为更短的子问题，学习求解子问题，再组合子问题的解。

Result: (i) SFT场景：课程学习仅需2^{O(√log T)}个监督token（次多项式），突破了直接模拟所需的Ω(T) token下界。(ii) RLVR场景：课程学习将参考模型的条件从覆盖完整序列长度T降为覆盖更短块长度B（B≪T），条件指数级减弱。

Conclusion: 递归分解与组合的课程学习策略在半自动机学习任务上具有显著的统计效率优势，为链式思维推理和组合泛化的理论基础提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Compositional generalization, the ability to solve complex problems by combining solutions to simpler sub-problems, is a fundamental capability of both natural and artificial intelligence, and a key mechanism underlying chain-of-thought reasoning. However, the theoretical underpinnings of compositional generalization remain poorly understood: when and why does decomposing a problem into parts yield more efficient learning than solving it directly? We study this question through the canonical problem of learning to simulate semiautomata (predicting the outcome of $T$ steps of sequential computation), a model that captures state tracking, regular language recognition, and modular arithmetic. We show that an autocurriculum-based approach building on Part I of this series, recursively decomposing longer sequences into shorter sub-problems, learning to solve them, and composing the solutions, achieves dramatically better statistical complexity than direct methods. (i) For a setting inspired by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) where the learner receives interactive feedback on intermediate states of the computation, curriculum facilitates learning from only $2^{\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\log T})}$ tokens of supervision; i.e., subpolynomial in the sequence length $T$, overcoming the $Ω(T)$ token barrier required by direct simulation. (ii) For a setting inspired by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where the learner improves a pre-trained reference model using an outcome verifier, we show that curriculum reduces the requirement on the reference model from coverage at the full sequence length $T$ to coverage at a shorter block length $B \ll T$, an exponentially weaker condition.

</details>


### [101] [Reduction of Probabilistic Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27737)
*Mauricio Montes,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将因子图约简技术迁移至化学反应网络（CRN），通过恢复Napp-Adams编译CRN中的因子图结构，在保持信念传播不动点的前提下大幅减小CRN规模，解决了概率生化计算中反应网络规模过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在细胞层面编程自适应行为需要生化系统实现概率计算，现有CRN方法虽能实现贝叶斯推理等算法，但编码这些算法需要规模过大的反应网络，且经典CRN约简技术不直接适用。

Method: 恢复Napp-Adams编译CRN中编码的因子图结构，将因子图约简结果迁移至CRN实现，在保持信念传播固定点不变的条件下约简反应网络。

Result: 获得了显著更小的CRN，同时保留了幸存变量上的信念传播不动点。

Conclusion: 因子图约简技术可有效迁移至CRN，为生化系统中的概率计算提供更高效的实现方案。

Abstract: Programming adaptive behaviors at the cellular level is a long-standing goal that raises the question of how probabilistic computation can be implemented in biochemical systems. Chemical reaction networks (CRNs) provide such a substrate and have been shown to realize probabilistic models, including hidden Markov models and factor graphs, with dynamics reproducing Bayesian inference and belief propagation. However, encoding these algorithms typically requires prohibitively large reaction networks, and classical CRN reduction techniques do not directly apply. By recovering the factor graph structure encoded in Napp--Adams-compiled CRNs, we transport recent factor-graph reduction results to their chemical implementations, obtaining significantly smaller CRNs while preserving the belief-propagation fixed points on surviving variables.

</details>


### [102] [The Weakest Link Tells It All: Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via Learnable Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27739)
*Tianyu Jia,Yue Fang,Hongxin Ding,Rihong Qiu,Zhibang Yang,Zhijing Wu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCA框架，通过可学习的信用分配机制，在仅使用最终答案监督信号的情况下训练过程奖励模型，基于"最弱链路分配"原则，将过程奖励建模转化为多实例学习问题，采用Softmax加权求和池化技术，在多个任务上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果监督的过程奖励模型虽能利用最终答案正确性进行可扩展训练，但存在信用分配难题——难以将结果归因到具体的推理步骤。现有均匀分配或因果分配方法均无法锚定步骤的正确性，阻碍了过程错误的识别。

Method: 1) 提出**可学习信用分配（LCA）**框架，联合学习信用分配与奖励建模；2) 遵循**最弱链路分配**原则；3) 将结果监督PRM形式化为多实例学习（MIL）问题；4) 引入**Softmax加权求和（SWS）**池化技术，专门处理推理状态间的强依赖性与冗余性；5) 在温和假设下证明了算法的贝叶斯一致性。

Result: 在多个任务和不同骨干模型上，LCA一致性地优于现有的最优结果监督PRM方法。

Conclusion: 通过可学习信用分配有效解决了结果监督PRM中的信用分配难题，为过程奖励模型提供了一种无需步骤级标注的可扩展训练范式。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by providing fine-grained feedback, yet training PRMs typically requires expensive stepwise annotations. Outcome-supervised PRMs offer a scalable alternative by learning from final-answer correctness alone, but this introduces a fundamental *credit assignment* challenge, i.e., attributing outcomes to responsible reasoning steps. Existing approaches rely on either uniform or causal assignment, both of which fail to anchor credit in step correctness and thus hinder process error identification.
  In this work, we propose Outcome-Supervised Process Reward Modeling via **L**earnable **C**redit **A**ssignment (**LCA**), an outcome-supervised PRM framework that jointly learns credit assignment and reward modeling under the principle of *Weakest Link Assignment: a reasoning chain is as strong as its weakest link*. To address mutual dependence between credit assignment and reward modeling, we formalize outcome-supervised PRM as a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) problem and introduce Softmax-Weighted-Sum (SWS) pooling, an MIL pooling technique tailored for strong dependence and redundancy among reasoning states. We prove Bayes consistency of our algorithm under mild assumptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **LCA** consistently outperforms state-of-the-art outcome-supervised PRMs across multiple tasks and backbones. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LCA.

</details>


### [103] [Flexformer: Flexible Linear Transformer with Learnable Attention Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27748)
*Haoran Zhang,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flexformer 通过将谱频率作为可训练参数，实现了全数据驱动的注意力核学习，在保持线性复杂度的同时提升了表达能力，在语言建模和序列分类任务上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于核的线性注意力通常使用固定或弱可学习的核函数，限制了表达能力和性能。现有方法无法充分灵活地学习适合特定任务的注意力核。

Method: 基于随机傅里叶特征的线性注意力机制，将谱频率作为可训练参数，提出静态和非静态两种变体，以数据驱动方式学习注意力核函数。

Result: 在语言建模和序列分类任务上持续优于基线；可从预训练Transformer蒸馏以恢复softmax注意力；核函数具有跨领域迁移性；在长序列任务上兼具高效性和竞争性性能。

Conclusion: Flexformer通过可学习的谱频率参数实现了灵活高效的线性注意力，非静态变体具有更强的表达能力，并能通过蒸馏方式逼近softmax注意力，具有良好的实用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Transformer models rely on attention mechanism to capture long-range dependencies but suffer from quadratic complexity, limiting their scalability to long sequences. Kernel-based linear attention reduces this complexity but typically relies on fixed or weakly learnable kernels, restricting expressiveness and performance. In this work, we propose Flexformer, a flexible linear Transformer that learns attention kernels in a fully data-driven manner. Flexformer builds on random Fourier feature-based linear attention and treats spectral frequencies as trainable parameters, enabling the model to learn a broad family of attention kernels.
  We develop both stationary and nonstationary variants, with the latter offering strictly greater expressiveness.
  Extensive experiments on language modeling and sequence classification demonstrate that Flexformer consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Flexformer can be effectively distilled from pretrained Transformers to recover softmax attention and exhibits strong kernel transferability across domains, achieving both high efficiency and competitive performance on long-sequence tasks.

</details>


### [104] [PerturbCellRL: Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27752)
*Dongxia Wu,Mingyu Li,Yuhui Zhang,Anurendra Kumar,Emma Lundberg,Serena Yeung-Levy,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PerturbCellRL框架，利用强化学习对预训练的单细胞转录组生成模型进行后训练，通过多个细胞级验证器（Pearson top-k相似度、RMSE top-k邻近度、DE Spearman、通路活性）作为奖励，确保生成的单个细胞在生物学上保持一致，在多个遗传和化学扰动基准测试中优于预训练模型，并在群体指标上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动生成模型虽然能改善群体水平的预测，但生成的单个细胞未被明确检查其生物学一致性，需要一种能保证单细胞层面生物学合理性的方法。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）框架，对预训练的单细胞转录组生成器进行后训练，使用一组细胞级验证器作为奖励信号，包括：Pearson top-k相似度、RMSE top-k邻近度、DE Spearman和通路活性验证器，其中通路活性验证器奖励那些通路响应与已知扰动生物学一致的细胞。

Result: 在多个遗传和化学扰动基准上，PerturbCellRL在奖励对齐的评估指标和保留评估指标上均优于预训练的流匹配生成器，同时在群体水平指标上与现有最先进方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 可信赖的单细胞预测可被框架化为验证器引导的生成对齐，超越匹配表达分布的范式，转向对单细胞扰动效应进行明确生物学一致性检查的预测。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation models can reduce costly wet-lab screening by predicting how cells respond transcriptionally to interventions. While recent generative models improve population-level prediction, individual generated cells are not explicitly checked for biological consistency. We introduce PerturbCellRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that post-trains a pretrained single-cell transcriptomic generator using a suite of cell-level verifiers as rewards. These verifiers define four rewards: Pearson top-k similarity, RMSE top-k proximity, DE Spearman, and Pathway activity. The Pathway activity verifier rewards cells whose pathway responses match known perturbation biology. We evaluate PerturbCellRL on multiple genetic and chemical perturbation benchmarks. Across these benchmarks, PerturbCellRL improves over the pretrained flow-matching generator on reward-aligned evaluation metrics and a held-out evaluation metric. Moreover, PerturbCellRL remains competitive with state-of-the-art methods on population-level metrics. Together, these results frame trustworthy single-cell prediction as verifier-guided generative alignment, moving beyond matching expression distributions toward predictions whose single-cell perturbation effects are explicitly checked for biological consistency.

</details>


### [105] [Layerwise Progressive Freezing: A Training Scaffold for Depth-Scalable Binary Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27759)
*Evan Gibson Smith,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StoMPP（随机掩码部分渐进二值化）方法，通过逐层从输入到输出渐进地将裁剪权重和激活替换为硬二值对应物，使用随机部分掩码和软刷新，实现完全不含STE的二值神经网络训练流程，并在多个网络架构和数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BNN训练被STE主导，但STE的前向/反向不匹配导致网络加深时精度严重下降。本文探索二值化在训练中何时何地执行这一正交维度，旨在消除STE带来的精度退化。

Method: 提出StoMPP方法，逐层从输入到输出渐进替换裁剪权重和激活为硬二值对应物，采用随机部分掩码和软刷新机制。作为独立训练规则时完全不含STE；与代理梯度结合时仅对冻结条目应用STE。关键发现：前向逐层渐进顺序可防止深度坍塌，而反向顺序导致性能接近随机。

Result: 独立训练下：ResNet-50 BNN在CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet上分别提升+18.0/+13.5/+3.8；与STE组合后提升达+27.1/+19.8/+17.7。效果在ResNet-18/34/50、MobileNetV2和BERT微调中一致。

Conclusion: 前向逐层渐进顺序对BNN训练至关重要，其不对称性源于激活引起的梯度阻塞——承诺的二值激活会切断上游梯度流，而顺序控制这些阻塞形成的时间。所有消融实验在无STE条件下进行，确保结果反映渐进机制本身而非与代理梯度的交互。

Abstract: Training binary neural networks (BNNs) from scratch is dominated by the straight-through estimator (STE), whose forward/backward mismatch produces severe accuracy degradation as networks deepen. We study an orthogonal axis: when and where binarization is enforced during training. We introduce StoMPP (Stochastic Masked Partial Progressive Binarization), which gradually replaces clipped weights and activations with their hard binary counterparts layer by layer from input to output, using stochastic partial masks with soft refresh. StoMPP delivers two complementary benefits. As a standalone training rule, it provides a fully STE-free procedure that improves over vanilla STE with gains that grow with depth (ResNet-50 BNN: +18.0/+13.5/+3.8 on CIFAR-10/100/ImageNet), and the pattern holds across ResNet-18/34/50, MobileNetV2, and BERT fine-tuning. Composed with surrogate gradients by applying STE only to frozen entries, it reaches +27.1/+19.8/+17.7 over vanilla STE on the same setting. Underlying both regimes is a single mechanistic finding: progression order is decisive. Forward layerwise progression prevents depth collapse, reverse progression collapses to near-chance, and binary-weight networks (without binary activations) are insensitive to order. We trace this asymmetry to activation-induced gradient blockades: a committed binary activation severs gradient flow upstream, and ordering controls when these blockades form. To isolate the progression's contribution from any benefit conferred by STE, we conduct all ablations in the STE-free regime; the resulting characterization (schedule, refresh, ordering, dynamics) thus reflects the progression itself rather than its interaction with surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [106] [RS-Diffuser: Risk-Sensitive Diffusion Planning with Distributional Value Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27766)
*Shiqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RS-Diffuser 提出了一种风险敏感的离线扩散规划框架，通过结合扩散轨迹生成与分布价值评估，利用条件风险价值等尾部感知目标在去噪过程中引导生成，使得单一模型可通过调整推理时的风险参数灵活实现风险规避、中性或寻求行为，在风险敏感的D4RL和机器人导航基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散规划器通常是风险中性的，可能忽略实际部署中罕见但灾难性的结果，而离线强化学习在安全关键应用中需要能够处理风险敏感场景。

Method: RS-Diffuser 包含三个组件：扩散轨迹生成器（未来状态轨迹）、逆动力学模型（动作解码）和蒙特卡洛分布评估器（通过分位数回归估计完整回报分布）。在去噪过程中，利用条件风险价值（CVaR）等尾部感知目标计算梯度，作为风险敏感引导信号。

Result: 在风险敏感的D4RL和机器人导航基准上，RS-Diffuser 在整体回报、最坏情况鲁棒性和安全违规减少方面均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 单一模型通过调整推理时的风险参数即可灵活实现不同的风险偏好行为（风险规避、中性、寻求），无需重新训练。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables policy learning from fixed datasets without additional environment interaction, making it appealing for safety-critical applications where online exploration is costly or unsafe. Diffusion-based decision-making methods have recently achieved strong performance in offline RL by modeling rich, multimodal trajectory distributions. However, existing diffusion planners are typically risk-neutral and therefore may overlook rare but catastrophic outcomes that are crucial in real-world deployment. In this work, we propose RS-Diffuser, a risk-sensitive offline diffusion planning framework that combines diffusion-based trajectory generation with distributional value critics. RS-Diffuser learns a diffusion planner over future state trajectories, a separate inverse dynamics model for action decoding, and a Monte Carlo distributional critic that estimates the full return distribution of candidate plans through quantile regression. At sampling time, we incorporate a risk-sensitive guidance signal into the denoising process, using gradients computed from tail-aware objectives such as Conditional Value at Risk to steer generation toward desired risk profiles. As a result, a single trained model can flexibly produce risk-averse, risk-neutral, or risk-seeking behaviors by changing only the inference-time risk parameter. Extensive experiments on risk-sensitive D4RL and risky robot navigation benchmarks demonstrate that RS-Diffuser achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving both overall return and worst-case robustness while reducing safety violations.

</details>


### [107] [Difference of Convex Programming in the Wasserstein Space with Applications to MMD Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27767)
*Clément Bonet,Pierre-Cyril Aubin-Frankowski,Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Wasserstein空间上的一类具有差值凸（DC）分解的泛函优化问题，将经典凸凹过程（CCCP）推广到该设置，并在光滑性和强凸性假设下证明了算法的几乎平稳性。重点针对最大均值差异（MMD）和能量距离（ED）泛函，开发了显式的Wasserstein DC分解，建立了局部收敛性，实验表明该方法比Wasserstein梯度下降更快更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率测度空间上的泛函优化在机器学习中应用广泛，但许多目标泛函沿Wasserstein测地线是非凸的，标准一阶方法的分析具有挑战性。本文旨在通过DC分解将CCCP方法推广到Wasserstein空间，以处理这类非凸优化问题。

Method: 将经典凸凹过程（CCCP）提升到Wasserstein空间；在光滑性和强凸性假设下证明迭代点的几乎平稳性；为MMD和ED泛函开发显式的Wasserstein DC分解并建立局部收敛性分析。

Result: 在光滑性与强凸性假设下，算法迭代点满足几乎平稳性；针对MMD和ED泛函成功构建了显式Wasserstein DC分解并证明了局部收敛性；实验表明精心设计的DC分解相比Wasserstein梯度下降收敛更快更稳定。

Conclusion: Wasserstein空间上的CCCP方法为处理非凸泛函优化提供了一种有效替代方案，尤其对MMD和ED这类目标函数，DC分解能带来更优的收敛性能。

Abstract: Optimizing functionals over the space of probability measures is now ubiquitous in machine learning. A widely used approach is to perform the optimization directly over the Wasserstein space, but many objective functionals of practical interest are non-convex along Wasserstein geodesics, making the analysis of standard first-order methods challenging. In this work, we study a class of objectives over the Wasserstein space that admit a difference-of-convex (DC) decomposition and we lift the classical convex-concave procedure (CCCP) to this setting. Under smoothness and strong convexity assumptions on the convex components of the decomposition, we prove almost stationarity along the iterates of the resulting algorithm. Our main focus is on the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) and the Energy Distance (ED) functionals, for which we develop explicit Wasserstein DC decompositions, and establish local convergence of the scheme under mild assumptions. Empirically, we show that well-chosen DC decompositions yield faster and more stable convergence than Wasserstein gradient descent on these MMD objectives.

</details>


### [108] [NormGuard: Reward-Preserving Norm Constraints in Flow-Matching Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27771)
*Tianlin Pan,Lianyu Pang,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习后训练会导致流式生成器每步速度范数膨胀5%-15%，降低感知质量。推理时重归一化无效（因膨胀已与模型权重共适应），而抑制范数膨胀不携带奖励信号。为此提出HingeLoss惩罚项，仅在速度范数超过参考值时激活，可兼容多种基础损失，在保持奖励的同时提升图像质量和真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练虽能提升流式生成器的奖励对齐，却常导致代理奖励无法捕捉的感知质量退化，亟需理解并解决该问题。

Method: 通过分析发现RL微调使每步速度范数\|v_θ\|相对参考值膨胀5%-15%的结构性特征。提出HingeLoss训练时惩罚项，仅在\|v_θ\|超过\|v_ref\|时激活，可与任何速度局域基础损失加性组合。

Result: 在两种基模型、三种后训练方法、两种奖励代理下，该方法一致提升了MLLM评判的图像质量和法医学真实性，同时保持奖励，且在少步推理场景下增益更显著，且不能由早停解释。

Conclusion: 推理时重归一化因膨胀已与权重共适应而无效；抑制范数膨胀在批次水平无一致一阶奖励信号；训练时干预是正确策略，所提HingeLoss有效解决了RL后训练的质量退化问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reward alignment of flow-based generators, but often degrades perceptual quality in ways that are not captured by the reward proxy. We identify a simple structural signature of this drift: across three post-training methods (NFT, AWM, DPO), RL fine-tuning inflates the per-step velocity norm $\|v_θ\|$ by $5\%$ to $15\%$ relative to the reference. A form of norm inflation has been studied in classifier-free guidance (CFG), where rescaling the velocity back to a reference norm at inference time can mitigate the resulting artifacts. However, this inference-time correction does not transfer cleanly to RL: rescaling $v_θ$ to match $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ at inference time neither improves reward nor fixes the quality degradation, because the inflation is co-adapted into the model weights. Furthermore, an adjoint sensitivity analysis shows that velocity magnitude rescaling carries no coherent first-order reward signal at the batch level, indicating that suppressing norm inflation is unlikely to remove a consistently reward-carrying component. Since inference-time renormalization fails while norm suppression carries no reward cost, training-time intervention is the appropriate strategy. Together, these findings motivate \methodname, a hinge penalty that activates only when $\|v_θ\|$ exceeds $\|v_{\text{ref}}\|$ and composes additively with any velocity-local base loss. Across two base models, three post-training methods, and two reward proxies, \methodname consistently improves MLLM-judged image quality and forensic realism while preserving reward, with gains that amplify under few-step inference and are not explained by early stopping.

</details>


### [109] [Accelerating Hierarchical Sparse Predictive Coding with Hybrid Amortized Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27802)
*Kazuhisa Fujita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了层级稀疏预测编码模型中的推理效率瓶颈，比较了四种推理方案（ISTA迭代、MFISTA加速、LISTA风格自编码器、混合方法），发现浅层LISTA初始化加少量校正迭代在重建质量、稀疏性和速度之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级稀疏预测编码模型具有计算和神经科学上的优势，但其实际应用受限于迭代潜变量推理的高昂计算成本，且随着层次加深问题愈发严重。本文旨在系统研究这一推理瓶颈，并寻找更高效的推理方案。

Method: 固定层级稀疏能量函数不变，在静态图像基准上比较四种推理方案：ISTA（经典迭代推理）、MFISTA（加速参考）、LISTA风格自编码器（结构感知的摊销推理）、混合方法（快速摊销初始化+少量能量校正步骤）。从重建质量、稀疏性、延迟和稳定性四个维度进行评估。

Result: 纯摊销推理速度快但质量有限；长迭代推理质量高但延迟大；混合方法（浅层LISTA初始化+少量校正迭代）显著优于纯摊销，同时比长迭代推理快得多，在速度与质量之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 针对层级稀疏预测编码模型的推理效率瓶颈，结构化摊销初始化结合少量能量校正的混合策略是一种实用且高效的折中方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical predictive coding provides an interpretable framework for perception as error-driven inference in multi-layer generative models, while sparse coding imposes parsimonious latent representations through explicit sparsity constraints. Their combination yields hierarchical sparse predictive coding models with appealing computational and neuroscientific properties, but practical use is often limited by the cost of iterative latent inference. In such models, each input may require many recurrent refinement steps before a useful sparse representation is obtained, and this burden becomes more severe as the hierarchy deepens. We study this bottleneck by holding the hierarchical sparse energy fixed and varying the inference procedure. The comparison includes four schemes: classical iterative inference based on ISTA, an accelerated MFISTA reference, structurally informed amortized inference using a LISTA-style bottom-up encoder adapted to the hierarchical model, and a hybrid method in which this fast amortized initialization is followed by a small number of corrective energy-based refinement steps. Under this shared objective, we measure reconstruction quality, sparsity, latency, and stability on static image benchmarks. The results show that a shallow LISTA-style initializer plus short corrective recurrence improves over pure amortization while remaining much faster than long iterative inference.

</details>


### [110] [Pepti-drift: Toxicity-Repulsive Drifting for Antigen-Conditioned Discrete Peptide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27824)
*Takashi Fujiwara,Hikaru Shindo,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pepti-drift是一个毒性感知的潜在空间精炼框架，通过单步抗原条件漂移生成肽候选物，在吸引抗原匹配结合肽的同时远离毒性区域，并采用预热策略平衡结合的吸引与毒性的排斥竞争目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多肽是一种兼具小分子化学可调性和大分子靶向特异性的有前景的治疗模式，但设计抗原特异性结合肽同时避免毒性仍是治疗性肽发现的主要挑战。

Method: 提出Pepti-drift框架，在肽嵌入空间中通过单步抗原条件漂移，吸引生成肽潜在表示朝向抗原匹配的结合肽，同时排斥远离毒性相关区域；针对结合促进与毒性特征重叠的问题，引入预热策略，先学习结合导向的吸引，再逐步增强毒性排斥。

Result: 该框架能够生成兼具抗原结合特异性和低毒性的肽候选物。

Conclusion: Pepti-drift通过毒性感知的潜在精炼和预热策略，有效解决了结合亲和力与毒性之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Peptides are a promising therapeutic modality that combine the chemical tunability of small molecules with the target specificity of macromolecular therapeutics. However, designing antigen-specific binding peptides while avoiding toxicity remains a major challenge for therapeutic peptide discovery. Here, we present Pepti-drift, a toxicity-aware latent refinement framework that generates peptide candidates through a single antigen-conditioned drift step.
